69: # 原薄实体膜模型使用的绘制厚度。
70: # 该厚度用于把片阻 Rs 换算为体电导率 sigma，并辅助 CST 建立可刻蚀薄层；

73: 
74: # =========================
75: # 12x12 表面 Ohmic Sheet 模板化拓扑优化设置
76: # =========================
77: link = "\n"
78: 
79: # 本版不再使用 84 bit 纯像素随机搜索，而是使用“物理模板参数化 GA”。
80: # 目标：尽量把 2--18 GHz 内 RL<-10 dB 的连续有效带宽推向 85% 覆盖率。
81: # 2--18 GHz 总宽度为 16 GHz，85% 对应 13.6 GHz。
82: #
83: # DNA 结构共 36 bit：
84: #   0:6    -> p
85: #   6:12   -> h
86: #   12:14  -> template_type
87: #   14:16  -> Y frame width
88: #   16:19  -> Y central window width
89: #   19:22  -> Y central window height
90: #   22:24  -> Y bridge width
91: #   24:27  -> Y slot type
92: #   27:29  -> X strip width
93: #   29:32  -> X central gap width
94: #   32:34  -> X end loading type
95: #   34:36  -> X vertical offset type
96: #
97: # 这样每个个体都对应一个物理上较合理的外框/窗口/桥接/条带图案，
98: # 避免 12x12 完全随机图案空间过大和过碎图案导致的求解器卡顿。
99: OPTIMIZE_P_H = True
100: TOPOLOGY_N = 12
101: SEED_N = 6
102: SEED_BITS_PER_LAYER = SEED_N * SEED_N
103: TEMPLATE_MODE = True
104: dna_size = 36
105: 
106: # 为降低吸收起始频率，需要适当放宽 p 和 h。
107: # 如需更激进地冲击低频，可把 P_MAX 提到 15.0、H_MAX 提到 11.2，
108: # 但建议先用当前范围跑通一轮。
109: P_MIN = 13.00
110: P_MAX = 14.80
111: H_MIN = 8.40
112: H_MAX = 11.00
113: 
114: # 当前 9x9 Ohmic Sheet + p,h 微扰最优结果，用作模板初始化中心。
115: FIXED_P = 13.18
116: FIXED_H = 8.866
117: 
118: # 这些 seed 变量仅用于兼容 fixed_best 等旧接口；模板优化时并不直接使用。
119: # 真正的 X/Y 图案由 decode() 中的 build_x_mask_template/build_y_mask_template 生成。
120: FIXED_MASK_X_SEED = [0] * (TOPOLOGY_N * TOPOLOGY_N)
121: FIXED_MASK_Y_SEED = [0] * (TOPOLOGY_N * TOPOLOGY_N)
122: 
123: # 默认先用中等规模测试。若能稳定跑完，可提高到 pop_size=10、iterations=8。
124: pop_size = 8
125: iterations = 5
126: p_m_start = 0.08
127: 
128: # 初始种群围绕一组接近最终 9x9 结构物理特征的模板参数进行扰动。
129: SEED_POPULATION_WITH_FIXED_BEST = True
130: SEED_PERTURB_COUNT = 8
131: SEED_PERTURB_BITS = 2
132: RANDOM_INDIVIDUAL_MAX_TRIALS = 500
133: ENABLE_MASK_PREFILTER = True
134: 
135: # 模板生成的图案通常比较规整，筛选阈值主要用于防止极端参数或变异个体。
136: MIN_KEPT_PIXELS_PER_LAYER = 12
137: MAX_KEPT_PIXELS_PER_LAYER = 140
138: MIN_TOTAL_KEPT_PIXELS = 45
139: MAX_TOTAL_KEPT_PIXELS = 230
140: MAX_ISOLATED_PIXELS_PER_LAYER = 6
141: MAX_CONNECTED_COMPONENTS_PER_LAYER = 8
142: 
143: # 85% 覆盖率目标。
144: TARGET_TOTAL_BAND = 16.0
145: TARGET_COVERAGE = 0.85
146: TARGET_CONTIGUOUS_BW = TARGET_TOTAL_BAND * TARGET_COVERAGE
147: TARGET_LOW_EDGE = 18.0 - TARGET_CONTIGUOUS_BW
148: 
149: INDIVIDUAL_TRACE_FILE = os.path.join(SAVE_PATH, "individual_trace_ohmic_sheet_12x12.txt")
150: SKIPPED_INDIVIDUAL_FILE = os.path.join(SAVE_PATH, "skipped_individuals_ohmic_sheet_12x12.txt")
151:

153: USE_MESH_ADAPTION_FOR_GA = False
154: RESULT_DATA_SAMPLES = 201

161: # =========================================================
162: # 分支1对比模式配置
163: # =========================================================
164: # RUN_MODE:
165: #   "fixed_best_ohmic" : 只用表面片阻层模型跑一次当前 12x12 最优图案，用于快速复核。
166: #   "optimize"         : 默认。沿用原遗传算法框架，但电阻膜统一改为表面 Ohmic Sheet。
167: #   "compare_fixed"    : 可选。重新对比薄实体膜与表面片阻层。
168: RUN_MODE = "optimize"
169: 
170: # 本轮优化统一采用 CST Ohmic Sheet 表面片阻层模型。
171: # 即：直接在 PET 表面生成二维片阻面，材料由 .OhmicSheetImpedance "60", "0" 定义。
172: OPTIMIZATION_FILM_MODEL = "ohmic_sheet"
173: 
174: # 当前 12x12 阶段最优结构，用于分支1固定结构对比
175: # FIXED_P/FIXED_H/FIXED_MASK_* 已在 12x12 配置区定义，这里不再重复定义。
176: 
177: # 分支1要对比的两种电阻膜建模方式
178: COMPARE_FILM_MODEL_TYPES = ["thin_solid", "ohmic_sheet"]
179: 
180: # CST Ohmic Sheet 材料名称。与原薄实体材料名称分开，便于在结果树和模型树中区分。
181: FILM_X_SHEET_NAME = "SMH188H_60_OhmicSheet"
182: FILM_Y_SHEET_NAME = "SMH188H_60_OhmicSheet"
183: 
184: # 片阻层模型创建方式：
185: #   "shape_from_helper_face"：根据你在 CST 2025 History List 中获得的命令：
186: #       Solid.CreateShapeFromFaces "solid2", "component1", "material1"
187: #   先创建一个极薄的临时辅助 brick，选取其外表面，再用 CreateShapeFromFaces
188: #   从被选中的 face 生成真正的 Ohmic Sheet 面对象，最后删除临时 brick。
189: #
190: # 这样生成的是 CST 认可的 “Ohmic sheet surf. imp.” 面对象，
191: # 而不是厚度为 0 的实体 brick。
192: OHMIC_SHEET_GEOMETRY_METHOD = "shape_from_helper_face"
193: 
194: # 临时辅助 brick 厚度，单位 mm。该 brick 只用于提供可 Pick 的 face，
195: # 生成 sheet 后会被删除，不参与最终电磁仿真。
196: HELPER_BRICK_THICKNESS = 1e-4
197: 
198: # 由你在 CST 2025 History List 中提供的命令可知，目标片阻面对应的 Pick face id 为 6：
199: #     Pick.PickFaceFromId "component1:pet_y", "6"
200: # 因此 v6 默认两个方向的临时辅助面都使用 face id = 6。
201: # 如果 X 膜位置仍不正确，可在 CST 中对 pet_x 的目标表面执行 Pick Face，
202: # 再把 History List 中的 face id 填到 FACE_ID_X_PLUS_Y。
203: FACE_ID_X_PLUS_Y = "6"
204: FACE_ID_Y_PLUS_X = "6"
205: 
206: # 是否把生成的 sheet 对象加入 meshgroup1。
207: # 你手动测试的 History List 中出现了：
208: # Group.AddItem "solid$component1:solid2", "meshgroup1"
209: # 这一步不是创建 sheet 的核心命令。v4 默认关闭，以减少调试阶段的额外报错来源。
210: ADD_SHEET_TO_MESHGROUP = False
211: MESHGROUP_NAME = "meshgroup1"
212: 
213: # CST 2025 的 Ohmic Sheet 材料在不同小版本中的 VBA 字段可能不同。
214: # 你的 error_log 已经证明：Material 对象没有 .Impedance 方法。
215: # v6 不再写死 .Impedance，而是先尝试读取你手动导出的材料历史命令；
216: # 若没有外部历史命令文件，则自动尝试若干常见字段，并把失败信息写入 error_log。
217: #
218: # 如果 v6 的自动候选仍失败，请在 CST History List 中点击第 2 行
219: # "define material: material1" -> Edit...，复制完整 VBA 到下面这个文件：
220: #     F:\biyesheji\code\ohmic_sheet_material_history.txt
221: # 然后重新运行本脚本。脚本会自动把其中的 material1 改名为所需材料名。
222: OHMIC_MATERIAL_HISTORY_FILE = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "ohmic_sheet_material_history.txt")

226: # 学长原版 Type()
227: # =========================================================
228: def Type():
229:     sCommand = [
230:         "'set the units",
231:         'With Units',
232:         '    .Geometry "mm"',
233:         '    .Frequency "GHz"',
234:         '    .Voltage "V"',
235:         '    .Resistance "Ohm"',
236:         '    .Inductance "H"',
237:         '    .TemperatureUnit  "Kelvin"',
238:         '    .Time "ns"',
239:         '    .Current "A"',
240:         '    .Conductance "Siemens"',
241:         '    .Capacitance "F"',
242:         'End With',
243:         '',
244:         "'----------------------------------------------------------------------------",
245:         '',
246:         "'set the frequency range",
247:         'Solver.FrequencyRange "2", "18"',
248:         '',
249:         "'----------------------------------------------------------------------------",
250:         '',
251:         'Plot.DrawBox True',
252:         '',
253:         'With Background',
254:         '     .Type "Normal"',
255:         '     .Epsilon "1.0"',
256:         '     .Mu "1.0"',
257:         '     .Rho "1.204"',
258:         '     .ThermalType "Normal"',
259:         '     .ThermalConductivity "0.026"',
260:         '      .SpecificHeat "1005", "J/K/kg"',
261:         '     .XminSpace "0.0"',
262:         '     .XmaxSpace "0.0"',
263:         '     .YminSpace "0.0"',
264:         '     .YmaxSpace "0.0"',
265:         '     .ZminSpace "0.0"',
266:         '     .ZmaxSpace "0.0"',
267:         'End With',
268:         '',
269:         "' define Floquet port boundaries",
270:         '',
271:         'With FloquetPort',
272:         '     .Reset',
273:         '     .SetDialogTheta "0"',
274:         '     .SetDialogPhi "0"',
275:         '     .SetSortCode "+beta/pw"',
276:         '     .SetCustomizedListFlag "False"',
277:         '     .Port "Zmin"',
278:         '     .SetNumberOfModesConsidered "2"',
279:         '     .Port "Zmax"',
280:         '     .SetNumberOfModesConsidered "2"',
281:         'End With',
282:         '',
283:         'MakeSureParameterExists "theta", "0"',
284:         'SetParameterDescription "theta", "spherical angle of incident plane wave"',
285:         'MakeSureParameterExists "phi", "0"',
286:         'SetParameterDescription "phi", "spherical angle of incident plane wave"',
287:         '',
288:         "' define boundaries, the open boundaries define floquet port",
289:         '',
290:         'With Boundary',
291:         '     .Xmin "unit cell"',
292:         '     .Xmax "unit cell"',
293:         '     .Ymin "unit cell"',
294:         '     .Ymax "unit cell"',
295:         '     .Zmin "expanded open"',
296:         '     .Zmax "expanded open"',
297:         '     .Xsymmetry "none"',
298:         '     .Ysymmetry "none"',
299:         '     .Zsymmetry "none"',
300:         '     .XPeriodicShift "0.0"',
301:         '     .YPeriodicShift "0.0"',
302:         '     .ZPeriodicShift "0.0"',
303:         '     .PeriodicUseConstantAngles "False"',
304:         '     .SetPeriodicBoundaryAngles "theta", "phi"',
305:         '     .SetPeriodicBoundaryAnglesDirection "inward"',
306:         '     .UnitCellFitToBoundingBox "True"',
307:         '     .UnitCellDs1 "0.0"',
308:         '     .UnitCellDs2 "0.0"',
309:         '     .UnitCellAngle "90.0"',
310:         'End With',
311:         '',
312:         "' set tet mesh as default",
313:         '',
314:         'With Mesh',
315:         '     .MeshType "Tetrahedral"',
316:         'End With',
317:         '',
318:         "' FD solver excitation with incoming plane wave at Zmax",
319:         '',
320:         'With FDSolver',
321:         '     .Reset',
322:         '     .Stimulation "List", "List"',
323:         '     .ResetExcitationList',
324:         '     .AddToExcitationList "Zmax", "TE(0,0);TM(0,0)"',
325:         '     .LowFrequencyStabilization "False"',
326:         'End With',
327:         '',
328:         "'----------------------------------------------------------------------------",
329:         '',
330:         'With MeshSettings',
331:         '     .SetMeshType "Tet"',
332:         '     .Set "Version", 1%',
333:         'End With',
334:         '',
335:         'With Mesh',
336:         '     .MeshType "Tetrahedral"',
337:         'End With',
338:         '',
339:         "'set the solver type",
340:         'ChangeSolverType("HF Frequency Domain")',
341:         '',
342:         "'----------------------------------------------------------------------------"
343:     ]
344:     sCommand = link.join(sCommand)
345:     pj.model3d.add_to_history("use template: FSS, Metamaterial - Unit Cell_3.cfg", sCommand)

348: 
349: def create_pet_material():
350:     sCommand = f'''
351: With Material
352:      .Reset
353:      .Name "PET"
354:      .Folder ""
355:      .Type "Normal"
356:      .Epsilon "{PET_ER}"
357:      .Mu "1.0"
358:      .Sigma "0"
359:      .TanD "{PET_TAND}"
360:      .Create
361: End With
362: '''
363:     pj.model3d.add_to_history("define material: PET", sCommand)
364: 
365: 
366: def create_resistive_film_material():
367:     film_thickness_m = FILM_DRAW_THICKNESS * 1e-3
368: 
369:     sigma_x = 1.0 / (FILM_X_SHEET_RESISTANCE * film_thickness_m)
370:     sigma_y = 1.0 / (FILM_Y_SHEET_RESISTANCE * film_thickness_m)
371: 
372:     sCommand1 = f'''
373: With Material
374:      .Reset
375:      .Name "{FILM_X_NAME}"
376:      .Folder ""
377:      .Type "Normal"
378:      .Epsilon "1.0"
379:      .Mu "1.0"
380:      .Sigma "{sigma_x}"
381:      .TanD "0.0"
382:      .Create
383: End With
384: '''
385:     pj.model3d.add_to_history("define material: resistive film x", sCommand1)
386: 
387:     sCommand2 = f'''
388: With Material
389:      .Reset
390:      .Name "{FILM_Y_NAME}"
391:      .Folder ""
392:      .Type "Normal"
393:      .Epsilon "1.0"
394:      .Mu "1.0"
395:      .Sigma "{sigma_y}"
396:      .TanD "0.0"
397:      .Create
398: End With
399: '''
400:     pj.model3d.add_to_history("define material: resistive film y", sCommand2)
401:

404: # =========================================================
405: # 分支1新增：CST Ohmic Sheet / 无厚度片阻层材料

407: def _append_error_log(message):
408:     """把材料候选命令失败原因写入 error_log，便于定位 CST VBA 字段。"""
409:     try:
410:         with open(ERROR_LOG_FILE, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
411:             f.write(str(message) + "\n")
412:     except Exception:
413:         pass
414: 
415: 
416: def _replace_material_name_in_history(history_text, target_name, rs):
417:     """把从 CST History List 复制出来的 material1 材料定义改名为目标材料名。"""
418:     text = history_text
419:     text = text.replace('.Name "material1"', f'.Name "{target_name}"')
420:     text = text.replace('.Name "Material1"', f'.Name "{target_name}"')
421:     text = text.replace('{MATERIAL_NAME}', target_name)
422:     text = text.replace('{SHEET_RESISTANCE}', str(rs))
423:     return text
424: 
425: 
426: def _try_create_material_from_external_history(target_name, rs, title):
427:     """优先使用用户从 CST History List 复制出来的完整 Ohmic Sheet 材料命令。"""
428:     if not os.path.exists(OHMIC_MATERIAL_HISTORY_FILE):
429:         return False
430: 
431:     with open(OHMIC_MATERIAL_HISTORY_FILE, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
432:         history_text = f.read().strip()
433: 
434:     if not history_text:
435:         return False
436: 
437:     cmd = _replace_material_name_in_history(history_text, target_name, rs)
438:     pj.model3d.add_to_history(title + " from external CST history", cmd)
439:     return True
440: 
441: 
442: def _candidate_ohmic_material_commands(material_name, rs, color_rgb):
443:     """生成多个 CST Ohmic Sheet 材料定义候选。
444: 
445:     你当前的报错已经确认 .Impedance 不是 Material 对象的方法。
446:     CST 不同版本的 Ohmic Sheet 参数名可能存在差异，因此这里使用候选法。
447:     """
448:     r, g, b = color_rgb
449:     candidates = []
450: 
451:     field_blocks = [
452:         ("SurfaceImpedance_pair", f'     .SurfaceImpedance "{rs}", "0"\n'),
453:         ("SurfaceImpedance_single", f'     .SurfaceImpedance "{rs}"\n'),
454:         ("SetSurfaceImpedance_pair", f'     .SetSurfaceImpedance "{rs}", "0"\n'),
455:         ("SetSurfaceImpedance_single", f'     .SetSurfaceImpedance "{rs}"\n'),
456:         ("SheetResistance", f'     .SheetResistance "{rs}"\n'),
457:         ("SetSheetResistance", f'     .SetSheetResistance "{rs}"\n'),
458:         ("SurfaceResistance", f'     .SurfaceResistance "{rs}"\n'),
459:         ("Resistance", f'     .Resistance "{rs}"\n'),
460:         ("OhmicSheetResistance", f'     .OhmicSheetResistance "{rs}"\n'),
461:         ("SetOhmicSheetResistance", f'     .SetOhmicSheetResistance "{rs}"\n'),
462:         ("TypeOnly", ''),
463:     ]
464: 
465:     type_strings = ["Ohmic sheet", "Ohmic Sheet", "OhmicSheet"]
466: 
467:     for type_string in type_strings:
468:         for label, field in field_blocks:
469:             cmd = f'''
470: With Material
471:      .Reset
472:      .Name "{material_name}"
473:      .Folder ""
474:      .FrqType "all"
475:      .Type "{type_string}"
476:      .MaterialUnit "Frequency", "GHz"
477:      .MaterialUnit "Geometry", "mm"
478: {field}     .Colour "{r}", "{g}", "{b}"
479:      .Transparency "0"
480:      .Create
481: End With
482: '''
483:             candidates.append((f"{type_string} + {label}", cmd))
484: 
485:     return candidates
486: 
487: 
488: def _create_ohmic_sheet_material_one(material_name, rs, color_rgb, title):
489:     """创建 CST 2025 可识别的 Ohmic Sheet 材料。
490: 
491:     这个命令来自你在 CST 2025 History List 中重新创建材料后导出的 VBA：
492:         .Type "Lossy metal"
493:         .OhmicSheetImpedance "60", "0"
494:         .OhmicSheetFreq "0"
495: 
496:     注意：CST 界面里显示为 Impedance = 60 [Ohm/sq]，但 VBA 字段不是 .Impedance，
497:     而是 .OhmicSheetImpedance。
498:     """
499:     r, g, b = color_rgb
500:     sCommand = f"""
501: With Material
502:      .Reset
503:      .Name "{material_name}"
504:      .Folder ""
505:      .Rho "0"
506:      .ThermalType "Normal"
507:      .ThermalConductivity "0"
508:      .SpecificHeat "0", "J/K/kg"
509:      .DynamicViscosity "0"
510:      .UseEmissivity "True"
511:      .Emissivity "0"
512:      .MetabolicRate "0.0"
513:      .VoxelConvection "0.0"
514:      .BloodFlow "0"
515:      .MechanicsType "Unused"
516:      .SolarRadiationAbsorptionType "Opaque"
517:      .Absorptance "0.0"
518:      .UseSemiTransparencyCalculator "False"
519:      .SolarRadTransmittance "0.0"
520:      .SolarRadReflectance "0.0"
521:      .SolarRadSpecimenThickness "0.0"
522:      .SolarRadRefractiveIndex "1.0"
523:      .SolarRadAbsorptionCoefficient "0.0"
524:      .IntrinsicCarrierDensityModel "none"
525:      .FrqType "all"
526:      .Type "Lossy metal"
527:      .MaterialUnit "Frequency", "GHz"
528:      .MaterialUnit "Geometry", "mm"
529:      .MaterialUnit "Time", "ns"
530:      .MaterialUnit "Temperature", "degC"
531:      .OhmicSheetImpedance "{rs}", "0"
532:      .OhmicSheetFreq "0"
533:      .ReferenceCoordSystem "Global"
534:      .CoordSystemType "Cartesian"
535:      .NLAnisotropy "False"
536:      .NLAStackingFactor "1"
537:      .NLADirectionX "1"
538:      .NLADirectionY "0"
539:      .NLADirectionZ "0"
540:      .Colour "{r}", "{g}", "{b}"
541:      .Wireframe "False"
542:      .Reflection "False"
543:      .Allowoutline "True"
544:      .Transparentoutline "False"
545:      .Transparency "0"
546:      .Create
547: End With
548: """
549:     pj.model3d.add_to_history(title, sCommand)
550: 
551: 
552: def create_ohmic_sheet_material():
553:     """创建 CST Ohmic Sheet 材料。X/Y 膜片阻相同，因此默认共用同一种材料。"""
554:     created = set()
555:     material_specs = [
556:         (FILM_X_SHEET_NAME, FILM_X_SHEET_RESISTANCE, ("0", "1", "1"), "define material: ohmic sheet film x"),
557:         (FILM_Y_SHEET_NAME, FILM_Y_SHEET_RESISTANCE, ("0", "1", "1"), "define material: ohmic sheet film y"),
558:     ]
559:     for name, rs, color, title in material_specs:
560:         if name in created:
561:             continue
562:         _create_ohmic_sheet_material_one(name, rs, color, title)
563:         created.add(name)
564: 
565: # =========================================================
566: # 学长原版 gaoshang()

574: # =========================================================
575: # 学长原版 newc()
576: # =========================================================
577: def newc():
578:     sCommand = ['Component.New "component1" ']
579:     sCommand = link.join(sCommand)
580:     pj.model3d.add_to_history("new component: component1", sCommand)
581: 
582: # =========================================================
583: 
584: def create_pet_cross_substrates(p, h):
585:     tx = SUBSTRATE_X_THICKNESS
586:     ty = SUBSTRATE_Y_THICKNESS
587: 
588:     s1 = f'''
589: With Brick
590:      .Reset
591:      .Name "pet_x"
592:      .Component "component1"
593:      .Material "PET"
594:      .Xrange "-{p/2}", "{p/2}"
595:      .Yrange "-{tx/2}", "{tx/2}"
596:      .Zrange "0", "{h}"
597:      .Create
598: End With
599: '''
600:     pj.model3d.add_to_history("define brick: pet_x", s1)
601: 
602:     s2 = f'''
603: With Brick
604:      .Reset
605:      .Name "pet_y"
606:      .Component "component1"
607:      .Material "PET"
608:      .Xrange "-{ty/2}", "{ty/2}"
609:      .Yrange "-{p/2}", "{p/2}"
610:      .Zrange "0", "{h}"
611:      .Create
612: End With
613: '''
614:     pj.model3d.add_to_history("define brick: pet_y", s2)
615: 
616: # =========================================================
617: 
618: def create_full_cover_film(p, h):
619:     tx = SUBSTRATE_X_THICKNESS
620:     ty = SUBSTRATE_Y_THICKNESS
621:     df = FILM_DRAW_THICKNESS
622: 
623:     # X向切片：膜固定在 +Y 面
624:     s1 = f'''
625: With Brick
626:      .Reset
627:      .Name "film_x_full"
628:      .Component "component1"
629:      .Material "{FILM_X_NAME}"
630:      .Xrange "-{p/2}", "{p/2}"
631:      .Yrange "{tx/2}", "{tx/2 + df}"
632:      .Zrange "0", "{h}"
633:      .Create
634: End With
635: '''
636:     pj.model3d.add_to_history("define brick: film_x_full", s1)
637: 
638:     # Y向切片：膜固定在 +X 面
639:     s2 = f'''
640: With Brick
641:      .Reset
642:      .Name "film_y_full"
643:      .Component "component1"
644:      .Material "{FILM_Y_NAME}"
645:      .Xrange "{ty/2}", "{ty/2 + df}"
646:      .Yrange "-{p/2}", "{p/2}"
647:      .Zrange "0", "{h}"
648:      .Create
649: End With
650: '''
651:     pj.model3d.add_to_history("define brick: film_y_full", s2)
652: 
653: # =========================================================
654: 
655: def etch_to_I_shape_fixedcaps(p, h, w1, w2, cap_ratio):
656:     tx = SUBSTRATE_X_THICKNESS
657:     ty = SUBSTRATE_Y_THICKNESS
658:     df = FILM_DRAW_THICKNESS
659: 
660:     wx = min(w1, p - 0.2)
661:     wy = min(w2, p - 0.2)
662: 
663:     cap_h = cap_ratio * h
664:     z1 = cap_h
665:     z2 = h - cap_h
666: 
667:     if z2 <= z1:
668:         z1 = 0.35 * h
669:         z2 = 0.65 * h
670: 
671:     s1 = f'''
672: With Brick
673:      .Reset
674:      .Name "etch_x_left_mid"
675:      .Component "component1"
676:      .Material "Vacuum"
677:      .Xrange "-{p/2}", "-{wx/2}"
678:      .Yrange "{tx/2}", "{tx/2 + df}"
679:      .Zrange "{z1}", "{z2}"
680:      .Create
681: End With
682: '''
683:     pj.model3d.add_to_history("define brick: etch_x_left_mid", s1)
684: 
685:     s2 = f'''
686: With Brick
687:      .Reset
688:      .Name "etch_x_right_mid"
689:      .Component "component1"
690:      .Material "Vacuum"
691:      .Xrange "{wx/2}", "{p/2}"
692:      .Yrange "{tx/2}", "{tx/2 + df}"
693:      .Zrange "{z1}", "{z2}"
694:      .Create
695: End With
696: '''
697:     pj.model3d.add_to_history("define brick: etch_x_right_mid", s2)
698: 
699:     s3 = f'''
700: With Brick
701:      .Reset
702:      .Name "etch_y_bottom_mid"
703:      .Component "component1"
704:      .Material "Vacuum"
705:      .Xrange "{ty/2}", "{ty/2 + df}"
706:      .Yrange "-{p/2}", "-{wy/2}"
707:      .Zrange "{z1}", "{z2}"
708:      .Create
709: End With
710: '''
711:     pj.model3d.add_to_history("define brick: etch_y_bottom_mid", s3)
712: 
713:     s4 = f'''
714: With Brick
715:      .Reset
716:      .Name "etch_y_top_mid"
717:      .Component "component1"
718:      .Material "Vacuum"
719:      .Xrange "{ty/2}", "{ty/2 + df}"
720:      .Yrange "{wy/2}", "{p/2}"
721:      .Zrange "{z1}", "{z2}"
722:      .Create
723: End With
724: '''
725:     pj.model3d.add_to_history("define brick: etch_y_top_mid", s4)
726: 
727:     s5 = '''
728: Solid.Subtract "component1:film_x_full", "component1:etch_x_left_mid"
729: Solid.Subtract "component1:film_x_full", "component1:etch_x_right_mid"
730: Solid.Subtract "component1:film_y_full", "component1:etch_y_bottom_mid"
731: Solid.Subtract "component1:film_y_full", "component1:etch_y_top_mid"
732: '''
733:     pj.model3d.add_to_history("etch film to I-shape", s5)
734:

738: def set_ground_boundary():
739:     sCommand = [
740:         'With Boundary',
741:         '     .Xmin "unit cell"',
742:         '     .Xmax "unit cell"',
743:         '     .Ymin "unit cell"',
744:         '     .Ymax "unit cell"',
745:         '     .Zmin "electric"',
746:         '     .Zmax "expanded open"',
747:         '     .Xsymmetry "none"',
748:         '     .Ysymmetry "none"',
749:         '     .Zsymmetry "none"',
750:         '     .ApplyInAllDirections "False"',
751:         '     .OpenAddSpaceFactor "0.5"',
752:         '     .XPeriodicShift "0.0"',
753:         '     .YPeriodicShift "0.0"',
754:         '     .ZPeriodicShift "0.0"',
755:         '     .PeriodicUseConstantAngles "False"',
756:         '     .SetPeriodicBoundaryAngles "theta", "phi"',
757:         '     .SetPeriodicBoundaryAnglesDirection "inward"',
758:         '     .UnitCellFitToBoundingBox "True"',
759:         '     .UnitCellDs1 "0.0"',
760:         '     .UnitCellDs2 "0.0"',
761:         '     .UnitCellAngle "90.0"',
762:         'End With'
763:     ]
764:     sCommand = link.join(sCommand)
765:     pj.model3d.add_to_history("define grounded boundaries", sCommand)
766: 
767: 
768: def set_fast_solver():
769:     sCommand = [
770:         'Mesh.SetCreator "High Frequency"',
771:         '',
772:         'With FDSolver',
773:         '     .Reset',
774:         '     .SetMethod "Tetrahedral", "General purpose"',
775:         '     .OrderTet "Second"',
776:         '     .OrderSrf "First"',
777:         '     .Stimulation "Zmax", "All"',
778:         '     .ResetExcitationList',
779:         '     .AddToExcitationList "Zmax", "TE(0,0);TM(0,0)"',
780:         '     .AutoNormImpedance "False"',
781:         '     .NormingImpedance "50"',
782:         '     .ModesOnly "False"',
783:         '     .ConsiderPortLossesTet "True"',
784:         '     .LowFrequencyStabilization "False"',
785:         '     .Type "Auto"',
786:         f'     .MeshAdaptionTet "{str(USE_MESH_ADAPTION_FOR_GA)}"',
787:         '     .StoreAllResults "False"',
788:         '     .StoreResultsInCache "False"',
789:         '     .UseDoublePrecision "False"',
790:         '     .UseParallelization "True"',
791:         '     .MaxCPUs "1024"',
792:         '     .MaximumNumberOfCPUDevices "2"',
793:         '     .RemoveAllStopCriteria "Tet"',
794:         '     .AddStopCriterion "All S-Parameters", "0.02", "2", "Tet", "True"',
795:         f'     .SetNumberOfResultDataSamples "{RESULT_DATA_SAMPLES}"',
796:         'End With',
797:         '',
798:         'With MeshSettings',
799:         '     .SetMeshType "Tet"',
800:         '     .Set "Version", 1%',
801:         '     .Set "StepsPerWaveNear", "3"',
802:         '     .Set "StepsPerWaveFar", "3"',
803:         '     .Set "PhaseErrorNear", "0.05"',
804:         '     .Set "PhaseErrorFar", "0.05"',
805:         '     .Set "CellsPerWavelengthPolicy", "automatic"',
806:         '     .Set "StepsPerBoxNear", "4"',
807:         '     .Set "StepsPerBoxFar", "1"',
808:         '     .Set "UseRatioLimit", "0"',
809:         '     .Set "RatioLimit", "100"',
810:         '     .Set "MinStep", "0"',
811:         'End With',
812:         '',
813:         'With Mesh',
814:         '     .MeshType "Tetrahedral"',
815:         '     .SetCreator "High Frequency"',
816:         'End With'
817:     ]
818:     sCommand = link.join(sCommand)
819:     pj.model3d.add_to_history("define fast FD solver and mesh", sCommand)

914: # GA辅助函数

916: def reconnect_de():
917:     global de
918:     try:
919:         de = cst.interface.DesignEnvironment()
920:     except Exception:
921:         pass
922: 
923: 
924: def safe_new_mws():
925:     global de
926:     try:
927:         return de.new_mws()
928:     except Exception:
929:         reconnect_de()
930:         return de.new_mws()
931: 
932: #--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
933: def bits_to_int(bits):
934:     return int("".join(str(int(b)) for b in bits), 2)
935: 
936: 
937: def clamp_int(v, lo, hi):
938:     return max(lo, min(hi, int(v)))
939: 
940: 
941: def build_y_mask_template(frame_w, window_w, window_h, bridge_w, slot_type, template_type):
942:     """生成 12x12 Y 向片阻模板。
943: 
944:     Y 膜在前期场分布中承担主要欧姆损耗，因此模板以“大外框 + 中央窗口 + 桥接通道”
945:     为核心，用于增加低频长电流路径，同时通过边缘槽调节高频端阻抗匹配。
946:     """
947:     n = TOPOLOGY_N
948:     m = np.ones((n, n), dtype=int)
949: 
950:     frame_w = clamp_int(frame_w, 1, 4)
951:     bridge_w = clamp_int(bridge_w, 1, 4)
952:     window_w = clamp_int(window_w, 2, 9)
953:     window_h = clamp_int(window_h, 2, 9)
954: 
955:     # 中央矩形窗口。先挖空，再恢复中心竖向/横向桥。
956:     cx = n // 2
957:     cy = n // 2
958:     x0 = clamp_int(cx - window_w // 2, frame_w, n - frame_w - 1)
959:     x1 = clamp_int(x0 + window_w, frame_w + 1, n - frame_w)
960:     y0 = clamp_int(cy - window_h // 2, frame_w, n - frame_w - 1)
961:     y1 = clamp_int(y0 + window_h, frame_w + 1, n - frame_w)
962:     m[y0:y1, x0:x1] = 0
963: 
964:     # 恢复外框。
965:     m[:frame_w, :] = 1
966:     m[-frame_w:, :] = 1
967:     m[:, :frame_w] = 1
968:     m[:, -frame_w:] = 1
969: 
970:     # 中心桥，保证长路径连通，避免形成过多孤立岛。
971:     bx0 = clamp_int(cx - bridge_w // 2, 0, n - 1)
972:     bx1 = clamp_int(bx0 + bridge_w, 1, n)
973:     by0 = clamp_int(cy - bridge_w // 2, 0, n - 1)
974:     by1 = clamp_int(by0 + bridge_w, 1, n)
975:     m[:, bx0:bx1] = 1
976:     m[by0:by1, :] = 1
977: 
978:     # 按模板类型添加侧向窗口/保留块，形成多尺度电流路径。
979:     if template_type == 0:
980:         # 接近 9x9 最终结构：四个侧窗 + 中央桥。
981:         pass
982:     elif template_type == 1:
983:         # 更强外框，减小中心窗口，利于低频。
984:         pad = 2
985:         m[pad:pad+2, pad:pad+2] = 0
986:         m[pad:pad+2, n-pad-2:n-pad] = 0
987:         m[n-pad-2:n-pad, pad:pad+2] = 0
988:         m[n-pad-2:n-pad, n-pad-2:n-pad] = 0
989:     elif template_type == 2:
990:         # 增强上下横向损耗带。
991:         m[2:4, :] = 1
992:         m[n-4:n-2, :] = 1
993:         m[2:4, 3:n-3] = 0
994:         m[n-4:n-2, 3:n-3] = 0
995:         m[2:4, bx0:bx1] = 1
996:         m[n-4:n-2, bx0:bx1] = 1
997:     else:
998:         # 稍微稀疏：扩大侧向窗口，避免整体过密导致高频端变差。
999:         m[2:4, 2:5] = 0
1000:         m[2:4, n-5:n-2] = 0
1001:         m[n-4:n-2, 2:5] = 0
1002:         m[n-4:n-2, n-5:n-2] = 0
1003: 
1004:     # 边缘槽：通过对称缺口调节高频端。
1005:     st = int(slot_type) % 8
1006:     slot_len = 2 + (st % 3)  # 2--4
1007:     if st in [1, 3, 5, 7]:
1008:         # 上下边中心开槽，但不破坏中心桥。
1009:         c0 = max(frame_w, cx - slot_len // 2)
1010:         c1 = min(n - frame_w, c0 + slot_len)
1011:         m[:frame_w, c0:c1] = 0
1012:         m[-frame_w:, c0:c1] = 0
1013:     if st in [2, 3, 6, 7]:
1014:         # 左右边中心开槽。
1015:         r0 = max(frame_w, cy - slot_len // 2)
1016:         r1 = min(n - frame_w, r0 + slot_len)
1017:         m[r0:r1, :frame_w] = 0
1018:         m[r0:r1, -frame_w:] = 0
1019:     if st in [4, 5, 6, 7]:
1020:         # 四角附近小槽，增加边缘多谐振。
1021:         m[frame_w:frame_w+1, frame_w+1:frame_w+3] = 0
1022:         m[frame_w:frame_w+1, n-frame_w-3:n-frame_w-1] = 0
1023:         m[n-frame_w-1:n-frame_w, frame_w+1:frame_w+3] = 0
1024:         m[n-frame_w-1:n-frame_w, n-frame_w-3:n-frame_w-1] = 0
1025: 
1026:     # 再次保证中心桥与外框基础连通。
1027:     m[:, bx0:bx1] = 1
1028:     m[by0:by1, :] = 1
1029:     return m.astype(int).tolist()
1030: 
1031: 
1032: def build_x_mask_template(strip_w, gap_w, end_load, vert_offset, template_type):
1033:     """生成 12x12 X 向片阻模板。
1034: 
1035:     X 膜主要用于耦合和阻抗修正。模板采用中部横向侧块/条带，并允许中央开缝、
1036:     端部加载和轻微垂直偏移，以调节高频端和主谐振位置。
1037:     """
1038:     n = TOPOLOGY_N
1039:     m = np.zeros((n, n), dtype=int)
1040: 
1041:     strip_w = clamp_int(strip_w, 1, 4)
1042:     gap_w = clamp_int(gap_w, 0, 8)
1043:     end_load = clamp_int(end_load, 0, 3)
1044:     offset_map = {0: -1, 1: 0, 2: 1, 3: 2}
1045:     off = offset_map.get(int(vert_offset) % 4, 0)
1046: 
1047:     cy = n // 2 + off
1048:     r0 = clamp_int(cy - strip_w // 2, 0, n - 1)
1049:     r1 = clamp_int(r0 + strip_w, 1, n)
1050: 
1051:     if gap_w <= 0:
1052:         m[r0:r1, :] = 1
1053:     else:
1054:         cx = n // 2
1055:         g0 = clamp_int(cx - gap_w // 2, 1, n - 1)
1056:         g1 = clamp_int(g0 + gap_w, 1, n - 1)
1057:         m[r0:r1, :g0] = 1
1058:         m[r0:r1, g1:] = 1
1059: 
1060:     # 端部加载：增加等效电长度，有利于低频，但保持中心开缝。
1061:     if end_load >= 1:
1062:         rr0 = max(0, r0 - 1)
1063:         rr1 = min(n, r1 + 1)
1064:         m[rr0:rr1, :2] = 1
1065:         m[rr0:rr1, -2:] = 1
1066:     if end_load >= 2:
1067:         rr0 = max(0, r0 - 2)
1068:         rr1 = min(n, r1 + 2)
1069:         m[rr0:rr1, :1] = 1
1070:         m[rr0:rr1, -1:] = 1
1071:     if end_load >= 3:
1072:         # 添加上下次级短条带，增加高频耦合通道。
1073:         if r0 - 3 >= 0:
1074:             m[r0-3:r0-2, 2:n-2] = 1
1075:         if r1 + 2 <= n:
1076:             m[r1+1:r1+2, 2:n-2] = 1
1077: 
1078:     if template_type == 1:
1079:         # 轻微增加中心两侧加载。
1080:         mid = n // 2
1081:         m[max(0, r0-1):min(n, r1+1), mid-3:mid-2] = 1
1082:         m[max(0, r0-1):min(n, r1+1), mid+2:mid+3] = 1
1083:     elif template_type == 2:
1084:         # 更稀疏，保留左右端块，降低过强高频反射风险。
1085:         mid = n // 2
1086:         m[:, mid-1:mid+1] = 0
1087:     elif template_type == 3:
1088:         # 双条带结构。
1089:         if r0 - 2 >= 0:
1090:             m[r0-2:r0-1, :max(1, n//2-gap_w//2)] = 1
1091:             m[r0-2:r0-1, min(n-1, n//2+gap_w//2):] = 1
1092:         if r1 + 1 <= n:
1093:             m[r1:r1+1, :max(1, n//2-gap_w//2)] = 1
1094:             m[r1:r1+1, min(n-1, n//2+gap_w//2):] = 1
1095: 
1096:     return m.astype(int).tolist()
1097: 
1098: 
1099: def flatten_mask(mask):
1100:     return [int(v) for row in mask for v in row]
1101: 
1102: 
1103: def template_params_from_individual(individual):
1104:     bits = [int(x) for x in individual]
1105:     if len(bits) != dna_size:
1106:         raise ValueError(f"Invalid DNA length: got {len(bits)}, expected {dna_size}")
1107: 
1108:     p_raw = bits_to_int(bits[0:6])
1109:     h_raw = bits_to_int(bits[6:12])
1110:     p = P_MIN + p_raw / 63.0 * (P_MAX - P_MIN)
1111:     h = H_MIN + h_raw / 63.0 * (H_MAX - H_MIN)
1112: 
1113:     template_type = bits_to_int(bits[12:14])
1114:     y_frame_w = 1 + bits_to_int(bits[14:16])                 # 1--4
1115:     y_window_w = 2 + bits_to_int(bits[16:19])                # 2--9
1116:     y_window_h = 2 + bits_to_int(bits[19:22])                # 2--9
1117:     y_bridge_w = 1 + bits_to_int(bits[22:24])                # 1--4
1118:     y_slot_type = bits_to_int(bits[24:27])                   # 0--7
1119:     x_strip_w = 1 + bits_to_int(bits[27:29])                 # 1--4
1120:     x_gap_w = bits_to_int(bits[29:32])                       # 0--7
1121:     x_end_load = bits_to_int(bits[32:34])                    # 0--3
1122:     x_vert_offset = bits_to_int(bits[34:36])                 # 0--3
1123: 
1124:     return {
1125:         "p": round(p, 4),
1126:         "h": round(h, 4),
1127:         "template_type": template_type,
1128:         "y_frame_w": y_frame_w,
1129:         "y_window_w": y_window_w,
1130:         "y_window_h": y_window_h,
1131:         "y_bridge_w": y_bridge_w,
1132:         "y_slot_type": y_slot_type,
1133:         "x_strip_w": x_strip_w,
1134:         "x_gap_w": x_gap_w,
1135:         "x_end_load": x_end_load,
1136:         "x_vert_offset": x_vert_offset,
1137:     }
1138: 
1139: 
1140: def decode(individual):
1141:     """解码模板化个体，返回 p、h 以及两个完整 12x12 mask 的一维展开列表。"""
1142:     params = template_params_from_individual(individual)
1143:     mask_x = build_x_mask_template(
1144:         strip_w=params["x_strip_w"],
1145:         gap_w=params["x_gap_w"],
1146:         end_load=params["x_end_load"],
1147:         vert_offset=params["x_vert_offset"],
1148:         template_type=params["template_type"],
1149:     )
1150:     mask_y = build_y_mask_template(
1151:         frame_w=params["y_frame_w"],
1152:         window_w=params["y_window_w"],
1153:         window_h=params["y_window_h"],
1154:         bridge_w=params["y_bridge_w"],
1155:         slot_type=params["y_slot_type"],
1156:         template_type=params["template_type"],
1157:     )
1158:     return params["p"], params["h"], flatten_mask(mask_x), flatten_mask(mask_y)
1159: 
1160: 
1161: def expand_seed_to_mask(seed):
1162:     """兼容旧接口。
1163: 
1164:     模板模式下 decode() 已经返回完整 12x12 mask 的一维展开列表；
1165:     如果传入长度为 144，则直接还原为 12x12 矩阵。
1166:     若传入长度为 36，则仍按 6x6 seed 镜像扩展。
1167:     """
1168:     if len(seed) == TOPOLOGY_N * TOPOLOGY_N:
1169:         return [list(map(int, seed[i * TOPOLOGY_N:(i + 1) * TOPOLOGY_N])) for i in range(TOPOLOGY_N)]
1170: 
1171:     if len(seed) != SEED_BITS_PER_LAYER:
1172:         raise ValueError(f"Topology requires full {TOPOLOGY_N}x{TOPOLOGY_N} mask or {SEED_N}x{SEED_N} seed, got length {len(seed)}")
1173: 
1174:     seed_rows = [seed[i * SEED_N:(i + 1) * SEED_N] for i in range(SEED_N)]
1175:     if TOPOLOGY_N == 2 * SEED_N:
1176:         top_rows = [row + row[::-1] for row in seed_rows]
1177:         mask = top_rows + top_rows[::-1]
1178:     elif TOPOLOGY_N == 2 * SEED_N - 1:
1179:         top_rows = [row + row[-2::-1] for row in seed_rows]
1180:         mask = top_rows + top_rows[-2::-1]
1181:     else:
1182:         raise ValueError(f"Unsupported topology/seed combination: N={TOPOLOGY_N}, SEED_N={SEED_N}")
1183:     return mask
1184: 
1185: 
1186: def template_summary(individual):
1187:     try:
1188:         p = template_params_from_individual(individual)
1189:         return ", ".join(f"{k}={v}" for k, v in p.items())
1190:     except Exception:
1191:         return ""
1192: 
1193: def bitstring(individual):
1194:     return "".join(str(int(x)) for x in individual)
1195: 
1196: 
1197: def mask_to_compact_string(mask):
1198:     return "/".join("".join(str(int(v)) for v in row) for row in mask)
1199: 
1200: 
1201: def count_isolated_pixels(mask):
1202:     """统计 4 邻域意义下的孤立保留像素。"""
1203:     n = len(mask)
1204:     m = len(mask[0]) if n else 0
1205:     iso = 0
1206:     for i in range(n):
1207:         for j in range(m):
1208:             if mask[i][j] != 1:
1209:                 continue
1210:             has_neighbor = False
1211:             for di, dj in [(-1, 0), (1, 0), (0, -1), (0, 1)]:
1212:                 ii, jj = i + di, j + dj
1213:                 if 0 <= ii < n and 0 <= jj < m and mask[ii][jj] == 1:
1214:                     has_neighbor = True
1215:                     break
1216:             if not has_neighbor:
1217:                 iso += 1
1218:     return iso
1219: 
1220: 
1221: def count_connected_components(mask):
1222:     """统计 4 邻域连通分量数，用于过滤过碎图案。"""
1223:     n = len(mask)
1224:     m = len(mask[0]) if n else 0
1225:     visited = [[False] * m for _ in range(n)]
1226:     comp = 0
1227: 
1228:     for i in range(n):
1229:         for j in range(m):
1230:             if mask[i][j] != 1 or visited[i][j]:
1231:                 continue
1232:             comp += 1
1233:             stack = [(i, j)]
1234:             visited[i][j] = True
1235:             while stack:
1236:                 x, y = stack.pop()
1237:                 for dx, dy in [(-1, 0), (1, 0), (0, -1), (0, 1)]:
1238:                     xx, yy = x + dx, y + dy
1239:                     if 0 <= xx < n and 0 <= yy < m and (not visited[xx][yy]) and mask[xx][yy] == 1:
1240:                         visited[xx][yy] = True
1241:                         stack.append((xx, yy))
1242:     return comp
1243: 
1244: 
1245: def mask_quality_report(mask):
1246:     kept = int(sum(sum(row) for row in mask))
1247:     isolated = count_isolated_pixels(mask)
1248:     components = count_connected_components(mask)
1249:     return {
1250:         "kept": kept,
1251:         "isolated": isolated,
1252:         "components": components,
1253:     }
1254: 
1255: 
1256: def prefilter_individual(individual):
1257:     """
1258:     在进入 CST 前过滤明显不适合表面 Ohmic Sheet 求解的坏图案。
1259:     返回: (ok, reason, report_dict)
1260:     """
1261:     if not ENABLE_MASK_PREFILTER:
1262:         p, h, sx, sy = decode(individual)
1263:         mx = expand_seed_to_mask(sx)
1264:         my = expand_seed_to_mask(sy)
1265:         return True, "prefilter_disabled", {
1266:             "p": p, "h": h, "mask_x": mx, "mask_y": my,
1267:             "x": mask_quality_report(mx), "y": mask_quality_report(my),
1268:         }
1269: 
1270:     p, h, sx, sy = decode(individual)
1271:     mx = expand_seed_to_mask(sx)
1272:     my = expand_seed_to_mask(sy)
1273:     rx = mask_quality_report(mx)
1274:     ry = mask_quality_report(my)
1275:     total = rx["kept"] + ry["kept"]
1276: 
1277:     reasons = []
1278:     for label, r in [("X", rx), ("Y", ry)]:
1279:         if r["kept"] < MIN_KEPT_PIXELS_PER_LAYER:
1280:             reasons.append(f"{label}_too_few_kept={r['kept']}")
1281:         if r["kept"] > MAX_KEPT_PIXELS_PER_LAYER:
1282:             reasons.append(f"{label}_too_many_kept={r['kept']}")
1283:         if r["isolated"] > MAX_ISOLATED_PIXELS_PER_LAYER:
1284:             reasons.append(f"{label}_too_many_isolated={r['isolated']}")
1285:         if r["components"] > MAX_CONNECTED_COMPONENTS_PER_LAYER:
1286:             reasons.append(f"{label}_too_many_components={r['components']}")
1287: 
1288:     if total < MIN_TOTAL_KEPT_PIXELS:
1289:         reasons.append(f"total_too_few_kept={total}")
1290:     if total > MAX_TOTAL_KEPT_PIXELS:
1291:         reasons.append(f"total_too_many_kept={total}")
1292: 
1293:     report = {
1294:         "p": p, "h": h,
1295:         "mask_x_seed": sx, "mask_y_seed": sy,
1296:         "mask_x": mx, "mask_y": my,
1297:         "x": rx, "y": ry, "total_kept": total,
1298:     }
1299: 
1300:     if reasons:
1301:         return False, ";".join(reasons), report
1302:     return True, "ok", report
1303: 
1304: 
1305: def log_individual_trace(generation, index, status, individual, reason="", report=None, metrics=None, cst_file=""):
1306:     """记录每个个体的状态，便于定位卡住的 CST 个体。"""
1307:     try:
1308:         p, h, sx, sy = decode(individual)
1309:         mx = expand_seed_to_mask(sx)
1310:         my = expand_seed_to_mask(sy)
1311:         if report is None:
1312:             report = {
1313:                 "x": mask_quality_report(mx),
1314:                 "y": mask_quality_report(my),
1315:                 "total_kept": int(sum(sum(r) for r in mx) + sum(sum(r) for r in my)),
1316:             }
1317:         metric_str = ""
1318:         if metrics is not None:
1319:             metric_str = (
1320:                 f"	score={metrics.get('score', '')}"
1321:                 f"	bandwidth={metrics.get('bandwidth', '')}"
1322:                 f"	min_rl={metrics.get('min_rl', '')}"
1323:             )
1324:         line = (
1325:             f"gen={generation}	idx={index}	status={status}	reason={reason}	"
1326:             f"bitstring={bitstring(individual)}	template={template_summary(individual)}	p={p}	h={h}	"
1327:             f"mask_x_seed={sx}	mask_y_seed={sy}	"
1328:             f"mask_x={mask_to_compact_string(mx)}	mask_y={mask_to_compact_string(my)}	"
1329:             f"x_report={report.get('x')}	y_report={report.get('y')}	"
1330:             f"total_kept={report.get('total_kept')}	cst_file={cst_file}{metric_str}\n"
1331:         )
1332:         with open(INDIVIDUAL_TRACE_FILE, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
1333:             f.write(line)
1334:     except Exception as e:
1335:         with open(INDIVIDUAL_TRACE_FILE, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
1336:             f.write(f"gen={generation}\tidx={index}\tstatus=trace_failed\terror={repr(e)}\n")
1337: 
1338: 
1339: def etch_mask_x_12x12(p, h, mask6):
1340:     tx = SUBSTRATE_X_THICKNESS
1341:     df = FILM_DRAW_THICKNESS
1342: 
1343:     cell_x = p / 6.0
1344:     cell_z = h / 6.0
1345: 
1346:     idx = 0
1347:     for i in range(6):
1348:         for j in range(6):
1349:             # mask6[i][j] == 1 表示保留
1350:             # mask6[i][j] == 0 表示蚀刻掉
1351:             if mask6[i][j] == 0:
1352:                 x1 = -p / 2 + j * cell_x
1353:                 x2 = x1 + cell_x
1354:                 z1 = i * cell_z
1355:                 z2 = z1 + cell_z
1356: 
1357:                 name = f"etch_x_{idx}"
1358:                 s = f'''
1359: With Brick
1360:      .Reset
1361:      .Name "{name}"
1362:      .Component "component1"
1363:      .Material "Vacuum"
1364:      .Xrange "{x1}", "{x2}"
1365:      .Yrange "{tx/2}", "{tx/2 + df}"
1366:      .Zrange "{z1}", "{z2}"
1367:      .Create
1368: End With
1369: '''
1370:                 pj.model3d.add_to_history(f"define brick: {name}", s)
1371: 
1372:                 sub = f'''
1373: Solid.Subtract "component1:film_x_full", "component1:{name}"
1374: '''
1375:                 pj.model3d.add_to_history(f"subtract: {name}", sub)
1376: 
1377:                 idx += 1
1378: 
1379: #--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

1385:     cell_z = h / 6.0
1386: 
1387:     idx = 0
1388:     for i in range(6):
1389:         for j in range(6):
1390:             # mask6[i][j] == 1 表示保留
1391:             # mask6[i][j] == 0 表示蚀刻掉
1392:             if mask6[i][j] == 0:
1393:                 y1 = -p / 2 + j * cell_y
1394:                 y2 = y1 + cell_y
1395:                 z1 = i * cell_z
1396:                 z2 = z1 + cell_z
1397: 
1398:                 name = f"etch_y_{idx}"
1399:                 s = f'''
1400: With Brick
1401:      .Reset
1402:      .Name "{name}"
1403:      .Component "component1"
1404:      .Material "Vacuum"
1405:      .Xrange "{ty/2}", "{ty/2 + df}"
1406:      .Yrange "{y1}", "{y2}"
1407:      .Zrange "{z1}", "{z2}"
1408:      .Create
1409: End With

1415: '''
1416:                 pj.model3d.add_to_history(f"subtract: {name}", sub)

1423: # 分支1新增：直接按 mask 创建片阻层图案，不再先全覆盖再布尔刻蚀

1425: def _add_sheet_to_meshgroup_if_needed(sheet_name):
1426:     """把生成的 sheet 对象加入 meshgroup1。该步骤不是必需的，但与你手动测试的 History 保持一致。"""
1427:     if not ADD_SHEET_TO_MESHGROUP:
1428:         return
1429: 
1430:     s = f'''
1431: On Error Resume Next
1432: Group.New "{MESHGROUP_NAME}"
1433: Group.AddItem "solid$component1:{sheet_name}", "{MESHGROUP_NAME}"
1434: On Error GoTo 0
1435: '''
1436:     pj.model3d.add_to_history(f"add sheet to mesh group: {sheet_name}", s)
1437: 
1438: 
1439: def _delete_helper_solid(helper_name):
1440:     """删除用于取面的临时辅助实体。"""
1441:     s = f'''
1442: On Error Resume Next
1443: Pick.ClearAllPicks
1444: Solid.Delete "component1:{helper_name}"
1445: On Error GoTo 0
1446: '''
1447:     pj.model3d.add_to_history(f"delete helper solid: {helper_name}", s)
1448: 
1449: 
1450: def _create_ohmic_sheet_from_helper_face(sheet_name, helper_name, material, face_id):
1451:     """从临时辅助实体的指定 face 生成 Ohmic Sheet 面对象。"""
1452:     # 这是根据你在 CST 2025 History List 第12行获得的核心命令改写的：
1453:     # Solid.CreateShapeFromFaces "solid2", "component1", "material1"
1454:     s = f'''
1455: Pick.ClearAllPicks
1456: Pick.PickFaceFromId "component1:{helper_name}", "{face_id}"
1457: Solid.CreateShapeFromFaces "{sheet_name}", "component1", "{material}"
1458: Pick.ClearAllPicks
1459: '''
1460:     pj.model3d.add_to_history(f"make ohmic sheet from helper face: {sheet_name}", s)
1461:     _add_sheet_to_meshgroup_if_needed(sheet_name)
1462:     _delete_helper_solid(helper_name)
1463: 
1464: 
1465: def create_surface_sheet_pixel_x(name, material, x1, x2, z1, z2):
1466:     """在 X 向 PET 切片的 +Y 表面创建一个二维片阻像素。
1467: 
1468:     方法：
1469:     1. 在 PET 表面内侧创建一个极薄的临时辅助 brick；
1470:     2. 选中该 brick 的 +Y face；
1471:     3. 用 Solid.CreateShapeFromFaces 生成真正的 Ohmic Sheet；
1472:     4. 删除临时辅助 brick。
1473: 
1474:     最终保留下来的对象是 sheet，不是有厚度实体膜。
1475:     """
1476:     y0 = SUBSTRATE_X_THICKNESS / 2.0
1477:     eps = HELPER_BRICK_THICKNESS
1478:     helper_name = f"helper_{name}"
1479: 
1480:     # 为了让 +Y face 正好落在 PET 表面 y=y0，辅助 brick 放在 y0-eps 到 y0。
1481:     s_helper = f'''
1482: With Brick
1483:      .Reset
1484:      .Name "{helper_name}"
1485:      .Component "component1"
1486:      .Material "Vacuum"
1487:      .Xrange "{x1}", "{x2}"
1488:      .Yrange "{y0 - eps}", "{y0}"
1489:      .Zrange "{z1}", "{z2}"
1490:      .Create
1491: End With
1492: '''
1493:     pj.model3d.add_to_history(f"create helper solid for x-sheet: {helper_name}", s_helper)
1494:     _create_ohmic_sheet_from_helper_face(name, helper_name, material, FACE_ID_X_PLUS_Y)
1495: 
1496: 
1497: def create_surface_sheet_pixel_y(name, material, y1, y2, z1, z2):
1498:     """在 Y 向 PET 切片的 +X 表面创建一个二维片阻像素。
1499: 
1500:     方法：
1501:     1. 在 PET 表面内侧创建一个极薄的临时辅助 brick；
1502:     2. 选中该 brick 的 +X face；
1503:     3. 用 Solid.CreateShapeFromFaces 生成真正的 Ohmic Sheet；
1504:     4. 删除临时辅助 brick。
1505: 
1506:     最终保留下来的对象是 sheet，不是有厚度实体膜。
1507:     """
1508:     x0 = SUBSTRATE_Y_THICKNESS / 2.0
1509:     eps = HELPER_BRICK_THICKNESS
1510:     helper_name = f"helper_{name}"
1511: 
1512:     # 为了让 +X face 正好落在 PET 表面 x=x0，辅助 brick 放在 x0-eps 到 x0。
1513:     s_helper = f'''
1514: With Brick
1515:      .Reset
1516:      .Name "{helper_name}"
1517:      .Component "component1"
1518:      .Material "Vacuum"
1519:      .Xrange "{x0 - eps}", "{x0}"
1520:      .Yrange "{y1}", "{y2}"
1521:      .Zrange "{z1}", "{z2}"
1522:      .Create
1523: End With
1524: '''
1525:     pj.model3d.add_to_history(f"create helper solid for y-sheet: {helper_name}", s_helper)
1526:     _create_ohmic_sheet_from_helper_face(name, helper_name, material, FACE_ID_Y_PLUS_X)
1527: 
1528: 
1529: def create_patterned_ohmic_sheet_film(p, h, mask_x, mask_y):
1530:     '''按任意 N x N mask 直接生成保留区域的二维 Ohmic Sheet 片阻像素。默认 N=9。'''
1531:     n_x = len(mask_x)
1532:     n_y = len(mask_y)
1533:     if n_x != n_y:
1534:         raise ValueError(f"mask_x and mask_y must have the same size, got {n_x} and {n_y}")
1535:     if n_x == 0 or any(len(row) != n_x for row in mask_x) or any(len(row) != n_y for row in mask_y):
1536:         raise ValueError("mask_x/mask_y must be square matrices")
1537: 
1538:     n = n_x
1539:     cell_x = p / float(n)
1540:     cell_y = p / float(n)
1541:     cell_z = h / float(n)
1542: 
1543:     idx = 0
1544:     for i in range(n):
1545:         for j in range(n):
1546:             if mask_x[i][j] == 1:
1547:                 x1 = -p / 2 + j * cell_x
1548:                 x2 = x1 + cell_x
1549:                 z1 = i * cell_z
1550:                 z2 = z1 + cell_z
1551:                 create_surface_sheet_pixel_x(f"sheet_x_{idx}", FILM_X_SHEET_NAME, x1, x2, z1, z2)
1552:                 idx += 1
1553: 
1554:     idx = 0
1555:     for i in range(n):
1556:         for j in range(n):
1557:             if mask_y[i][j] == 1:
1558:                 y1 = -p / 2 + j * cell_y
1559:                 y2 = y1 + cell_y
1560:                 z1 = i * cell_z
1561:                 z2 = z1 + cell_z
1562:                 create_surface_sheet_pixel_y(f"sheet_y_{idx}", FILM_Y_SHEET_NAME, y1, y2, z1, z2)
1563:                 idx += 1
1564: 
1565: 
1566: def build_resistive_film_by_model_type(p, h, mask_x, mask_y, film_model_type):
1567:     '''根据 film_model_type 建立电阻膜图案。'''
1568:     if film_model_type == "thin_solid":
1569:         create_resistive_film_material()
1570:         create_full_cover_film(p, h)
1571:         raise NotImplementedError("12x12 version is intended for ohmic_sheet model. Thin-solid etching is not used here.")
1572:     elif film_model_type == "ohmic_sheet":
1573:         create_ohmic_sheet_material()
1574:         create_patterned_ohmic_sheet_film(p, h, mask_x, mask_y)
1575:     else:
1576:         raise ValueError(f"Unknown film_model_type: {film_model_type}")

1578: #--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1579: def _find_effective_intervals(freqs, rls, threshold=-10.0):
1580:     below = rls <= threshold
1581:     intervals = []
1582:     start = None
1583:     for i, ok in enumerate(below):
1584:         if ok and start is None:
1585:             start = i
1586:         if (not ok or i == len(below) - 1) and start is not None:
1587:             end = i - 1 if not ok else i
1588:             if end >= start:
1589:                 intervals.append((start, end))
1590:             start = None
1591:     return intervals
1592: 
1593: 
1594: def extract_metrics(cst_file):
1595:     result = cst.results.ProjectFile(cst_file, allow_interactive=True)
1596:     s11 = result.get_3d().get_result_item(r"1D Results\S-Parameters\SZmax(1),Zmax(1)")
1597:     data = s11.get_data()
1598: 
1599:     freqs = []
1600:     rls = []
1601:     for row in data:
1602:         if len(row) < 2:
1603:             continue
1604:         freq = float(row[0])
1605:         mag = max(abs(row[1]), 1e-12)
1606:         rl = 20 * np.log10(mag)
1607:         freqs.append(freq)
1608:         rls.append(float(rl))
1609: 
1610:     if not freqs:
1611:         return {
1612:             "score": 0.0, "bandwidth": 0.0, "coverage": 0.0, "min_rl": 0.0,
1613:             "f_low": 0.0, "f_high": 0.0, "contiguous_bw": 0.0,
1614:             "total_effective_bw": 0.0,
1615:         }
1616: 
1617:     freqs = np.array(freqs, dtype=float)
1618:     rls = np.array(rls, dtype=float)
1619:     min_rl = float(np.min(rls))
1620: 
1621:     intervals = _find_effective_intervals(freqs, rls, threshold=-10.0)
1622:     if intervals:
1623:         best_start, best_end = max(intervals, key=lambda t: freqs[t[1]] - freqs[t[0]])
1624:         f_low = float(freqs[best_start])
1625:         f_high = float(freqs[best_end])
1626:         contiguous_bw = max(0.0, f_high - f_low)
1627:     else:
1628:         f_low, f_high, contiguous_bw = 18.0, 2.0, 0.0
1629: 
1630:     # total_effective_bw 用点数估计，contiguous_bw 用最长连续区间估计。
1631:     below = rls <= -10.0
1632:     if len(freqs) > 1:
1633:         df = float(np.mean(np.diff(freqs)))
1634:     else:
1635:         df = 0.0
1636:     total_effective_bw = float(np.sum(below) * df)
1637:     coverage = contiguous_bw / TARGET_TOTAL_BAND
1638: 
1639:     # 只在有效区间内计算平均吸收裕量，避免深谷奖励过强。
1640:     if np.any(below):
1641:         mean_margin = float(np.mean(np.maximum(0.0, -10.0 - rls[below])))
1642:     else:
1643:         mean_margin = 0.0
1644: 
1645:     # 评分重点：最长连续有效带宽、低频起点、高频端保持和 85% 目标奖励。
1646:     score = 2.0 * contiguous_bw
1647: 
1648:     # 奖励低频边界下移。希望从 5.6 GHz 推向 4.4--5.0 GHz。
1649:     if contiguous_bw > 0:
1650:         score += 1.8 * max(0.0, 6.0 - f_low)
1651:         if f_low <= 5.0:
1652:             score += 2.5
1653:         if f_low <= TARGET_LOW_EDGE + 0.25:
1654:             score += 4.0
1655: 
1656:     # 惩罚高频端提前失效，鼓励一直延伸到 18 GHz 附近。
1657:     if f_high >= 17.8:
1658:         score += 2.5
1659:     else:
1660:         score -= 1.8 * max(0.0, 17.8 - f_high)
1661: 
1662:     # 达到或接近 85% 覆盖率给强奖励。
1663:     if contiguous_bw >= TARGET_CONTIGUOUS_BW:
1664:         score += 12.0 + 3.0 * (contiguous_bw - TARGET_CONTIGUOUS_BW)
1665:     else:
1666:         score += 1.0 * max(0.0, contiguous_bw - 12.3)
1667: 
1668:     # 吸收深度作为辅助，避免只追求 -10 dB 临界线；但不让深谷主导搜索。
1669:     score += 0.05 * min(abs(min_rl), 45.0)
1670:     score += 0.10 * min(mean_margin, 20.0)
1671: 
1672:     if contiguous_bw == 0:
1673:         score = max(0.0, (-min_rl - 3.0) * 0.02)
1674: 
1675:     return {
1676:         "score": float(score),
1677:         "bandwidth": float(contiguous_bw),
1678:         "coverage": float(coverage),
1679:         "min_rl": float(min_rl),
1680:         "f_low": float(f_low),
1681:         "f_high": float(f_high),
1682:         "contiguous_bw": float(contiguous_bw),
1683:         "total_effective_bw": float(total_effective_bw),
1684:         "mean_margin": float(mean_margin),
1685:     }
1686:

1697:             continue

1733: def remove_file_if_exists(path):
1734:     """删除已经存在的文件，避免 CST 保存或复制时因为同名文件报错。"""
1735:     try:
1736:         if path and os.path.exists(path):
1737:             os.remove(path)
1738:     except Exception:
1739:         pass

1741: 
1742: def make_run_tag():
1743:     """生成短时间标签，让临时 CST 文件不重名。"""
1744:     return time.strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S") + f"_{random.randint(1000, 9999)}"
1745: 
1746: #--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1747: def run_cst_for_params(p, h, mask_x_seed, mask_y_seed, film_model_type="thin_solid", cst_basename=None):
1748:     '''对指定参数运行一次 CST 仿真。
1749: 
1750:     v4 修改：
1751:     1. 每次运行使用不重名的临时 CST 文件，避免同名 .cst 已存在导致保存失败；
1752:     2. 在关键步骤加入 stage 标记，若失败可从 error_log.txt 直接判断卡在哪一步。
1753:     '''
1754:     global pj
1755:     stage = "new_mws"
1756:     pj = None
1757: 
1758:     try:
1759:         pj = safe_new_mws()
1760: 
1761:         stage = "template_and_materials"
1762:         Type()
1763:         create_pet_material()
1764:         newc()
1765: 
1766:         stage = "expand_mask"
1767:         mask_x = expand_seed_to_mask(mask_x_seed)
1768:         mask_y = expand_seed_to_mask(mask_y_seed)
1769: 
1770:         print(
1771:             f"Current parameters: "
1772:             f"p={p:.4f}, h={h:.4f}, "
1773:             f"film_model_type={film_model_type}, "
1774:             f"mask_x_seed={mask_x_seed}, mask_y_seed={mask_y_seed}"
1775:         )
1776: 
1777:         stage = "create_pet_substrates"
1778:         create_pet_cross_substrates(p, h)
1779: 
1780:         stage = f"build_resistive_film_{film_model_type}"
1781:         build_resistive_film_by_model_type(p, h, mask_x, mask_y, film_model_type)
1782: 
1783:         stage = "boundaries_and_solver_settings"
1784:         set_ground_boundary()
1785:         set_fast_solver()
1786: 
1787:         if cst_basename is None:
1788:             cst_basename = f"temp_{film_model_type}_{make_run_tag()}.cst"
1789:         cst_file = os.path.join(TEMP_DIR, cst_basename)
1790:         remove_file_if_exists(cst_file)
1791: 
1792:         stage = "run_solver"
1793:         print("Running CST solver...")
1794:         pj.model3d.run_solver()
1795: 
1796:         stage = "save_cst_project"
1797:         pj.save(cst_file)
1798: 
1799:         stage = "extract_metrics"
1800:         metrics = extract_metrics(cst_file)
1801:         return metrics, (p, h, mask_x_seed, mask_y_seed), cst_file
1802: 
1803:     except Exception as e:
1804:         raise RuntimeError(
1805:             f"CST run failed at stage='{stage}', film_model_type='{film_model_type}'. "
1806:             f"Original error: {repr(e)}"
1807:         ) from e
1808: 
1809:     finally:
1810:         try:
1811:             if pj is not None:
1812:                 pj.close()
1813:         except Exception:
1814:             pass
1815: 
1816: 
1817: def run_cst(individual, film_model_type=None):
1818:     '''保留原遗传算法接口：输入 72 bit individual，解码后调用 run_cst_for_params。'''
1819:     if film_model_type is None:
1820:         film_model_type = OPTIMIZATION_FILM_MODEL
1821: 
1822:     p, h, mask_x_seed, mask_y_seed = decode(individual)
1823:     return run_cst_for_params(
1824:         p=p,
1825:         h=h,
1826:         mask_x_seed=mask_x_seed,
1827:         mask_y_seed=mask_y_seed,
1828:         film_model_type=film_model_type,
1829:     )
1830: 
1831: 
1832: def int_to_bits(value, width):
1833:     return [int(b) for b in format(value, f"0{width}b")]
1834: 
1835: 
1836: def encode_template_params_to_individual(
1837:     p=FIXED_P,
1838:     h=FIXED_H,
1839:     template_type=0,
1840:     y_frame_w=1,
1841:     y_window_w=6,
1842:     y_window_h=7,
1843:     y_bridge_w=2,
1844:     y_slot_type=0,
1845:     x_strip_w=3,
1846:     x_gap_w=4,
1847:     x_end_load=2,
1848:     x_vert_offset=1,
1849: ):
1850:     """把模板参数编码为 36 bit individual。"""
1851:     p_raw = int(round((p - P_MIN) / (P_MAX - P_MIN) * 63.0))
1852:     h_raw = int(round((h - H_MIN) / (H_MAX - H_MIN) * 63.0))
1853:     p_raw = max(0, min(63, p_raw))
1854:     h_raw = max(0, min(63, h_raw))
1855: 
1856:     vals = [
1857:         (p_raw, 6),
1858:         (h_raw, 6),
1859:         (template_type, 2),
1860:         (y_frame_w - 1, 2),
1861:         (y_window_w - 2, 3),
1862:         (y_window_h - 2, 3),
1863:         (y_bridge_w - 1, 2),
1864:         (y_slot_type, 3),
1865:         (x_strip_w - 1, 2),
1866:         (x_gap_w, 3),
1867:         (x_end_load, 2),
1868:         (x_vert_offset, 2),
1869:     ]
1870:     ind = []
1871:     for value, width in vals:
1872:         max_v = (1 << width) - 1
1873:         value = max(0, min(max_v, int(value)))
1874:         ind.extend(int_to_bits(value, width))
1875:     if len(ind) != dna_size:
1876:         raise ValueError(f"Encoded DNA length mismatch: {len(ind)} vs {dna_size}")
1877:     return ind
1878: 
1879: 
1880: def encode_params_to_individual(p, h, mask_x_seed=None, mask_y_seed=None):
1881:     """兼容旧接口：模板版本忽略 mask_x_seed/mask_y_seed，只编码 p、h 和默认模板参数。"""
1882:     return encode_template_params_to_individual(p=p, h=h)
1883: 
1884: def perturb_individual(individual, n_bits=2):
1885:     child = individual.copy()
1886:     idxs = random.sample(range(dna_size), k=min(n_bits, dna_size))
1887:     for idx in idxs:
1888:         child[idx] = 1 - child[idx]
1889:     return child
1890: 
1891: 
1892: def initialize_population(n):
1893:     pop = []
1894: 
1895:     if SEED_POPULATION_WITH_FIXED_BEST:
1896:         fixed_ind = encode_params_to_individual(
1897:             FIXED_P,
1898:             FIXED_H,
1899:             FIXED_MASK_X_SEED,
1900:             FIXED_MASK_Y_SEED,
1901:         )
1902:         pop.append(fixed_ind)
1903: 
1904:         # 加入多个围绕当前最优结构的局部扰动个体。
1905:         # 只翻转 1 bit，优先保证图案不要突然变得过碎。
1906:         trial = 0
1907:         while len(pop) < min(n, 1 + SEED_PERTURB_COUNT) and trial < RANDOM_INDIVIDUAL_MAX_TRIALS:
1908:             trial += 1
1909:             child = perturb_individual(fixed_ind, SEED_PERTURB_BITS)
1910:             ok, reason, report = prefilter_individual(child)
1911:             if ok and child not in pop:
1912:                 pop.append(child)
1913:             elif not ok:
1914:                 with open(SKIPPED_INDIVIDUAL_FILE, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
1915:                     f.write(f"initial_seed_perturb_skipped\treason={reason}\tbitstring={bitstring(child)}\n")
1916: 
1917:     # 少量随机个体也要通过预筛选，避免 CST 被明显坏图案拖住。
1918:     trial = 0
1919:     while len(pop) < n and trial < RANDOM_INDIVIDUAL_MAX_TRIALS:
1920:         trial += 1
1921:         ind = [random.randint(0, 1) for _ in range(dna_size)]
1922:         ok, reason, report = prefilter_individual(ind)
1923:         if ok and ind not in pop:
1924:             pop.append(ind)
1925:         elif not ok:
1926:             with open(SKIPPED_INDIVIDUAL_FILE, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
1927:                 f.write(f"initial_random_skipped\treason={reason}\tbitstring={bitstring(ind)}\n")
1928: 
1929:     # 如果筛选太严格导致数量不够，则用当前最优结构的扰动补齐。
1930:     if SEED_POPULATION_WITH_FIXED_BEST:
1931:         fixed_ind = encode_params_to_individual(FIXED_P, FIXED_H, FIXED_MASK_X_SEED, FIXED_MASK_Y_SEED)
1932:         while len(pop) < n:
1933:             child = perturb_individual(fixed_ind, 1)
1934:             if child not in pop:
1935:                 pop.append(child)
1936:     else:
1937:         while len(pop) < n:
1938:             ind = [random.randint(0, 1) for _ in range(dna_size)]
1939:             if ind not in pop:
1940:                 pop.append(ind)
1941: 
1942:     return pop
1943: 
1944: 
1945: def evaluate_individual(individual, generation=None, index=None):
1946:     key = tuple(individual)
1947:     if key in cache:
1948:         metrics, params, cst_file = cache[key]
1949:         log_individual_trace(generation, index, "cache_hit", individual, metrics=metrics, cst_file=cst_file)
1950:         return cache[key]
1951: 
1952:     ok, reason, report = prefilter_individual(individual)
1953:     if not ok:
1954:         metrics = {"score": 0.0, "bandwidth": 0.0, "min_rl": 0.0}
1955:         p, h, sx, sy = decode(individual)
1956:         params = (p, h, sx, sy)
1957:         cst_file = ""
1958:         cache[key] = (metrics, params, cst_file)
1959:         log_individual_trace(generation, index, "skipped_prefilter", individual, reason=reason, report=report, metrics=metrics)
1960:         with open(SKIPPED_INDIVIDUAL_FILE, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
1961:             f.write(f"gen={generation}\tidx={index}\treason={reason}\tbitstring={bitstring(individual)}\n")
1962:         return cache[key]
1963: 
1964:     log_individual_trace(generation, index, "start_cst", individual, reason=reason, report=report)
1965: 
1966:     try:
1967:         metrics, params, cst_file = run_cst(individual)
1968:         log_individual_trace(generation, index, "finished_cst", individual, reason="ok", report=report, metrics=metrics, cst_file=cst_file)
1969:     except Exception as e:
1970:         with open(ERROR_LOG_FILE, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
1971:             f.write("\n" + "=" * 80 + "\n")
1972:             f.write(f"generation = {generation}, index = {index}\n")
1973:             f.write(f"individual = {individual}\n")
1974:             f.write(f"bitstring = {bitstring(individual)}\n")
1975:             f.write(f"prefilter_report = {report}\n")
1976:             f.write(repr(e) + "\n")
1977:             f.write(traceback.format_exc() + "\n")
1978: 
1979:         metrics = {"score": 0.0, "bandwidth": 0.0, "min_rl": 0.0}
1980:         p, h, sx, sy = decode(individual)
1981:         params = (p, h, sx, sy)
1982:         cst_file = ""
1983:         log_individual_trace(generation, index, "failed_cst", individual, reason=repr(e), report=report, metrics=metrics)
1984: 
1985:     cache[key] = (metrics, params, cst_file)
1986:     return cache[key]
1987: 
1988: 
1989: def evaluate_population(pop, generation=None):
1990:     fit_list = []
1991:     info_list = []
1992: 
1993:     for idx, ind in enumerate(pop):
1994:         print(f"\n--- Evaluating generation {generation}, individual {idx + 1}/{len(pop)} ---")
1995:         print(f"bitstring = {bitstring(ind)}")
1996:         metrics, params, cst_file = evaluate_individual(ind, generation=generation, index=idx)
1997:         fit_list.append(metrics["score"])
1998:         info_list.append((metrics, params, cst_file))
1999: 
2000:     return fit_list, info_list
2001: 
2002: 
2003: def select_parent(pop, fit_list):
2004:     positive = [max(0.0, x) for x in fit_list]
2005:     total = sum(positive)
2006: 
2007:     if total <= 0:
2008:         return random.choice(pop).copy()
2009: 
2010:     r = random.random() * total
2011:     s = 0.0
2012:     for ind, fit in zip(pop, positive):
2013:         s += fit
2014:         if r <= s:
2015:             return ind.copy()
2016: 
2017:     return pop[-1].copy()
2018: 
2019: 
2020: def crossover(father, mother):
2021:     # 通用双点交叉，仍适用于当前 72 bit；之后扩展到 12x12 时也更方便复用。
2022:     cross_point1 = random.randint(1, dna_size // 2 - 1)
2023:     cross_point2 = random.randint(dna_size // 2, dna_size - 1)
2024:     return father[:cross_point1] + mother[cross_point1:cross_point2] + father[cross_point2:]
2025: 
2026: 
2027: def mutate(individual, p_m):
2028:     child = individual.copy()
2029:     if random.random() < p_m:
2030:         mp1 = random.randint(0, dna_size - 1)
2031:         mp2 = random.randint(0, dna_size - 1)
2032:         child[mp1] = 1 - child[mp1]
2033:         child[mp2] = 1 - child[mp2]
2034:     return child
2035: 
2036: 
2037: def make_new_population(pop, fit_list, p_m):
2038:     elite_idx = int(np.argmax(fit_list))
2039:     elite = pop[elite_idx].copy()
2040: 
2041:     newpop = [elite]
2042:     trial = 0
2043:     while len(newpop) < pop_size and trial < RANDOM_INDIVIDUAL_MAX_TRIALS:
2044:         trial += 1
2045:         father = select_parent(pop, fit_list)
2046:         mother = select_parent(pop, fit_list)
2047:         child = crossover(father, mother)
2048:         child = mutate(child, p_m)
2049:         ok, reason, report = prefilter_individual(child)
2050:         if ok and child not in newpop:
2051:             newpop.append(child)
2052:         elif not ok:
2053:             with open(SKIPPED_INDIVIDUAL_FILE, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
2054:                 f.write(f"new_population_skipped\treason={reason}\tbitstring={bitstring(child)}\n")
2055: 
2056:     # 如果交叉/变异产生的合格新个体不足，则从精英附近做 1 bit 局部扰动补齐。
2057:     while len(newpop) < pop_size:
2058:         child = perturb_individual(elite, 1)
2059:         ok, reason, report = prefilter_individual(child)
2060:         if ok and child not in newpop:
2061:             newpop.append(child)
2062:         else:
2063:             # 极端情况下允许重复精英，保证程序继续运行。
2064:             if elite not in newpop or len(newpop) == 0:
2065:                 newpop.append(elite.copy())
2066:             elif len(newpop) < pop_size:
2067:                 newpop.append(elite.copy())
2068: 
2069:     return newpop
2070: 
2071: 
2072: def write_best_result(best_metrics, best_ind, best_params):
2073:     with open(BEST_RESULT_FILE, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
2074:         f.write(f"Best score: {best_metrics['score']}\n")
2075:         f.write(f"Best contiguous bandwidth( RL<-10 dB ): {best_metrics['bandwidth']} GHz\n")
2076:         f.write(f"Best coverage in 2-18 GHz: {best_metrics.get('coverage', 0.0) * 100.0} %\n")
2077:         f.write(f"Best total effective bandwidth estimate: {best_metrics.get('total_effective_bw', 0.0)} GHz\n")
2078:         f.write(f"Best low edge: {best_metrics.get('f_low', 0.0)} GHz\n")
2079:         f.write(f"Best high edge: {best_metrics.get('f_high', 0.0)} GHz\n")
2080:         f.write(f"Best min RL: {best_metrics['min_rl']} dB\n")
2081:         f.write(f"Best individual: {best_ind}\n")
2082:         f.write(f"Best template parameters: {template_summary(best_ind)}\n")
2083:         f.write(f"Best parameters (p, h, mask_x_flatten, mask_y_flatten): {best_params}\n")
2084:         f.write(f"Best CST file: {BEST_CST}\n")
2085:         f.write(f"Best S11 txt file: {BEST_S11_FILE}\n")
2086:         f.write(f"Best S11 png file: {BEST_S11_PNG}\n")
2087: 
2088: def optimize():
2089:     pop = initialize_population(pop_size)
2090: 
2091:     best_metrics = {"score": -1.0, "bandwidth": 0.0, "min_rl": 0.0}
2092:     best_ind = None
2093:     best_params = None
2094: 
2095:     with open(LOG_FILE, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
2096:         f.write("iteration\tindividual\ttemplate\tscore\tbandwidth\tcoverage\tmin_rl\tf_low\tf_high\ttotal_effective_bw\tp\th\tmask_x_flatten\tmask_y_flatten\tcst_file\n")
2097: 
2098:     for it in range(iterations):
2099:         current_pm = max(0.03, p_m_start - 0.008 * it)
2100:         print(f"\n===== Iteration {it} =====")
2101:         print(f"Current mutation rate = {current_pm:.3f}")
2102: 
2103:         fit_list, info_list = evaluate_population(pop, generation=it)
2104: 
2105:         for ind, fit, info in zip(pop, fit_list, info_list):
2106:             metrics, params, cst_file = info
2107:             print(
2108:                 f"score = {metrics['score']:.6f}, "
2109:                 f"bandwidth = {metrics['bandwidth']:.6f}, "
2110:                 f"coverage = {metrics.get('coverage', 0.0)*100:.2f}%, f_low = {metrics.get('f_low', 0.0):.3f}, f_high = {metrics.get('f_high', 0.0):.3f}, minRL = {metrics['min_rl']:.3f}"
2111:             )
2112: 
2113:             with open(LOG_FILE, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
2114:                 f.write(
2115:                     f"{it}\t{ind}\t{template_summary(ind)}\t{metrics['score']}\t{metrics['bandwidth']}\t"
2116:                     f"{metrics.get('coverage', 0.0)}\t{metrics['min_rl']}\t{metrics.get('f_low', 0.0)}\t"
2117:                     f"{metrics.get('f_high', 0.0)}\t{metrics.get('total_effective_bw', 0.0)}\t"
2118:                     f"{params[0]}\t{params[1]}\t{params[2]}\t{params[3]}\t{cst_file}\n"
2119:                 )
2120: 
2121:         idx = int(np.argmax(fit_list))
2122:         current_best_metrics, current_best_params, current_best_cst = info_list[idx]
2123: 
2124:         if current_best_metrics["score"] > best_metrics["score"]:
2125:             best_metrics = current_best_metrics
2126:             best_ind = pop[idx].copy()
2127:             best_params = current_best_params
2128: 
2129:             if current_best_cst and os.path.exists(current_best_cst):
2130:                 shutil.copyfile(current_best_cst, BEST_CST)
2131:                 export_s11_curve(current_best_cst, BEST_S11_FILE)
2132:                 export_s11_plot(current_best_cst, BEST_S11_PNG)
2133: 
2134:             write_best_result(best_metrics, best_ind, best_params)
2135: 
2136:         print(f"best score so far = {best_metrics['score']:.6f}")
2137:         print(f"best bandwidth so far = {best_metrics['bandwidth']:.6f} GHz, coverage = {best_metrics.get('coverage', 0.0)*100:.2f}%")
2138:         print(f"best min RL so far = {best_metrics['min_rl']:.3f} dB")
2139:         print(f"best params so far = {best_params}")
2140: 
2141:         pop = make_new_population(pop, fit_list, current_pm)
2142: 
2143:     print("\nOptimization finished.")
2144:     print(f"Best score = {best_metrics['score']}")
2145:     print(f"Best bandwidth = {best_metrics['bandwidth']} GHz, coverage = {best_metrics.get('coverage', 0.0)*100:.2f}%")
2146:     print(f"Best min RL = {best_metrics['min_rl']} dB")
2147:     print(f"Best params = {best_params}")
2148:     print(f"Best CST file saved to: {BEST_CST}")
2149:

2211: 
2212: def read_exported_s11_txt(path):
2213:     freq = []
2214:     rl = []
2215:     with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
2216:         next(f, None)
2217:         for line in f:
2218:             parts = line.strip().split()
2219:             if len(parts) < 3:
2220:                 continue
2221:             freq.append(float(parts[0]))
2222:             rl.append(float(parts[2]))
2223:     return np.array(freq), np.array(rl)

2233:         data_by_model[model] = (f, rl)
2234:         plt.plot(f, rl, label=model)
2235: 
2236:     plt.axhline(-10, linestyle="--")
2237:     plt.xlabel("Frequency (GHz)")
2238:     plt.ylabel("Reflection Loss RL (dB)")
2239:     plt.title("Comparison: thin solid film vs surface ohmic sheet")
2240:     plt.grid(True)

2244:     plt.close()

2247:         for model, (freq, rl) in data_by_model.items():
2248:             f.write(f"# model = {model}\n")
2249:             f.write("freq_GHz\trl_dB\n")
2250:             for ff, rr in zip(freq, rl):
2251:                 f.write(f"{ff}\t{rr}\n")

2327:
