4440: 
4441: def ensure_array_mask(mask):
4442:     return np.array(mask, dtype=int).reshape((TOPOLOGY_N, TOPOLOGY_N))
4443: 
4444: 
4445: def flatten_mask(mask):
4446:     return [int(v) for v in ensure_array_mask(mask).reshape(-1).tolist()]
4447: 
4448: 
4449: def matrix_to_string(mask):
4450:     m = ensure_array_mask(mask)
4451:     return "/".join("".join(str(int(v)) for v in row) for row in m)
4452: 
4453: 
4454: def mask_quality_report(mask):
4455:     m = ensure_array_mask(mask).tolist()
4456:     return {
4457:         "kept": int(sum(sum(row) for row in m)),
4458:         "isolated": count_isolated_pixels(m),
4459:         "components": count_connected_components(m),
4460:     }
4461: 
4462: 
4463: def remove_isolated_pixels(m):
4464:     m = ensure_array_mask(m).copy()
4465:     n = TOPOLOGY_N
4466:     for i in range(n):
4467:         for j in range(n):
4468:             if m[i, j] != 1:
4469:                 continue
4470:             cnt = 0
4471:             for di, dj in [(-1,0),(1,0),(0,-1),(0,1)]:
4472:                 ii, jj = i + di, j + dj
4473:                 if 0 <= ii < n and 0 <= jj < n and m[ii, jj] == 1:
4474:                     cnt += 1
4475:             if cnt == 0:
4476:                 m[i, j] = 0
4477:     return m
4478: 
4479: 
4480: def repair_x_mask(mask_x):
4481:     mx = ensure_array_mask(mask_x).copy()
4482:     mx = remove_isolated_pixels(mx)
4483: 
4484:     # Keep at least part of the validated 82% X family.
4485:     base_core = BEST82_X_MASK_12 == 1
4486:     if int(np.sum(mx & base_core)) < 18:
4487:         mx[base_core] = BEST82_X_MASK_12[base_core]
4488: 
4489:     # Avoid over-dense X that kills high-frequency edge.
4490:     if int(np.sum(mx)) > MAX_X_KEPT:
4491:         added = np.argwhere((mx == 1) & (BEST82_X_MASK_12 == 0))
4492:         np.random.shuffle(added)
4493:         for i, j in added:
4494:             if int(np.sum(mx)) <= MAX_X_KEPT:
4495:                 break
4496:             mx[i, j] = 0
4497: 
4498:     return mx.astype(int)
4499: 
4500: 
4501: def repair_y_mask(mask_y):
4502:     my = ensure_array_mask(mask_y).copy()
4503: 
4504:     # Hard skeleton always retained.
4505:     my[Y_HARD_KEEP] = 1
4506: 
4507:     # Remove isolated noise in soft regions.
4508:     my = remove_isolated_pixels(my)
4509:     my[Y_HARD_KEEP] = 1
4510: 
4511:     # Avoid too dense Y: preferentially restore some white windows from the 82% seed.
4512:     if int(np.sum(my)) > MAX_Y_KEPT:
4513:         candidates = np.argwhere((my == 1) & (BEST82_Y_MASK_12 == 0) & (~Y_HARD_KEEP))
4514:         np.random.shuffle(candidates)
4515:         for i, j in candidates:
4516:             if int(np.sum(my)) <= MAX_Y_KEPT:
4517:                 break
4518:             my[i, j] = 0
4519: 
4520:     # Avoid too sparse Y: restore validated seed pixels first.
4521:     if int(np.sum(my)) < MIN_Y_KEPT:
4522:         candidates = np.argwhere((my == 0) & (BEST82_Y_MASK_12 == 1))
4523:         np.random.shuffle(candidates)
4524:         for i, j in candidates:
4525:             if int(np.sum(my)) >= MIN_Y_KEPT:
4526:                 break
4527:             my[i, j] = 1
4528: 
4529:     my[Y_HARD_KEEP] = 1
4530:     return my.astype(int)
4531: 
4532: 
4533: def repair_masks(mask_x, mask_y):
4534:     return repair_x_mask(mask_x), repair_y_mask(mask_y)
4535: 
4536: 
4537: def make_individual(p=None, h=None, rsx=None, rsy=None, mask_x=None, mask_y=None, origin="seed"):
4538:     if p is None:
4539:         p = random.uniform(P_MIN, P_MAX)
4540:     if h is None:
4541:         h = random.uniform(H_MIN, H_MAX)
4542:     if rsx is None:
4543:         rsx = random.choice([
4544:             random.uniform(100, 160),
4545:             random.uniform(140, 210),
4546:             random.uniform(RS_X_MIN, RS_X_MAX),
4547:         ])
4548:     if rsy is None:
4549:         rsy = random.choice([
4550:             random.uniform(25, 48),
4551:             random.uniform(35, 65),
4552:             random.uniform(RS_Y_MIN, RS_Y_MAX),
4553:         ])
4554:     if mask_x is None:
4555:         mask_x = BEST82_X_MASK_12.copy()
4556:     if mask_y is None:
4557:         mask_y = BEST82_Y_MASK_12.copy()
4558: 
4559:     mx, my = repair_masks(mask_x, mask_y)
4560:     return {
4561:         "p": round(float(max(P_MIN, min(P_MAX, p))), 4),
4562:         "h": round(float(max(H_MIN, min(H_MAX, h))), 4),
4563:         "rsx": round(float(max(RS_X_MIN, min(RS_X_MAX, rsx))), 3),
4564:         "rsy": round(float(max(RS_Y_MIN, min(RS_Y_MAX, rsy))), 3),
4565:         "mask_x": mx.astype(int).tolist(),
4566:         "mask_y": my.astype(int).tolist(),
4567:         "origin": origin,
4568:     }
4569: 
4570: 
4571: def clone_individual(ind):
4572:     return {
4573:         "p": float(ind["p"]),
4574:         "h": float(ind["h"]),
4575:         "rsx": float(ind["rsx"]),
4576:         "rsy": float(ind["rsy"]),
4577:         "mask_x": [row[:] for row in ind["mask_x"]],
4578:         "mask_y": [row[:] for row in ind["mask_y"]],
4579:         "origin": ind.get("origin", "clone"),
4580:     }
4581: 
4582: 
4583: def individual_key(ind):
4584:     return (
4585:         round(float(ind["p"]), 4),
4586:         round(float(ind["h"]), 4),
4587:         round(float(ind["rsx"]), 3),
4588:         round(float(ind["rsy"]), 3),
4589:         tuple(flatten_mask(ind["mask_x"])),
4590:         tuple(flatten_mask(ind["mask_y"])),
4591:     )

4594: def bitstring(individual):
4595:     if isinstance(individual, dict):
4596:         return (
4597:             f"p={individual['p']},h={individual['h']},"
4598:             f"rsx={individual['rsx']},rsy={individual['rsy']},"
4599:             f"mx={matrix_to_string(individual['mask_x'])},"
4600:             f"my={matrix_to_string(individual['mask_y'])}"
4601:         )
4602:     return "".join(str(int(x)) for x in individual)
4603: 
4604: 
4605: def template_summary(individual):
4606:     if isinstance(individual, dict):
4607:         rx = mask_quality_report(individual["mask_x"])
4608:         ry = mask_quality_report(individual["mask_y"])
4609:         return (
4610:             f"origin={individual.get('origin','')}, "
4611:             f"p={individual['p']}, h={individual['h']}, "
4612:             f"Rs_x={individual['rsx']}, Rs_y={individual['rsy']}, "
4613:             f"X(kept={rx['kept']}, comp={rx['components']}), "
4614:             f"Y(kept={ry['kept']}, comp={ry['components']})"
4615:         )
4616:     return ""
4617: 
4618: 
4619: def decode(individual):
4620:     if isinstance(individual, dict):
4621:         return (
4622:             float(individual["p"]),
4623:             float(individual["h"]),
4624:             flatten_mask(individual["mask_x"]),
4625:             flatten_mask(individual["mask_y"]),
4626:         )
4627:     raise ValueError("Multi-template topology code expects dict individual")
4628: 
4629: 
4630: def prefilter_individual(individual):
4631:     p, h, sx, sy = decode(individual)
4632:     mx = expand_seed_to_mask(sx)
4633:     my = expand_seed_to_mask(sy)
4634:     rx = mask_quality_report(mx)
4635:     ry = mask_quality_report(my)
4636: 
4637:     reasons = []
4638:     if rx["kept"] < MIN_X_KEPT:
4639:         reasons.append(f"X_too_few_kept={rx['kept']}")
4640:     if rx["kept"] > MAX_X_KEPT:
4641:         reasons.append(f"X_too_many_kept={rx['kept']}")
4642:     if rx["components"] > MAX_X_COMPONENTS:
4643:         reasons.append(f"X_too_many_components={rx['components']}")
4644:     if rx["isolated"] > MAX_X_ISOLATED:
4645:         reasons.append(f"X_too_many_isolated={rx['isolated']}")
4646: 
4647:     if ry["kept"] < MIN_Y_KEPT:
4648:         reasons.append(f"Y_too_few_kept={ry['kept']}")
4649:     if ry["kept"] > MAX_Y_KEPT:
4650:         reasons.append(f"Y_too_many_kept={ry['kept']}")
4651:     if ry["components"] > MAX_Y_COMPONENTS:
4652:         reasons.append(f"Y_too_many_components={ry['components']}")
4653:     if ry["isolated"] > MAX_Y_ISOLATED:
4654:         reasons.append(f"Y_too_many_isolated={ry['isolated']}")
4655: 
4656:     report = {
4657:         "p": p, "h": h,
4658:         "rsx": individual["rsx"], "rsy": individual["rsy"],
4659:         "mask_x_seed": sx, "mask_y_seed": sy,
4660:         "mask_x": mx, "mask_y": my,
4661:         "x": rx, "y": ry,
4662:         "total_kept": rx["kept"] + ry["kept"],
4663:     }
4664:     if reasons:
4665:         return False, ";".join(reasons), report
4666:     return True, "ok", report
4667: 
4668: 
4669: def configure_rs_for_individual(individual):
4670:     global FILM_X_SHEET_RESISTANCE, FILM_Y_SHEET_RESISTANCE, FILM_X_SHEET_NAME, FILM_Y_SHEET_NAME
4671:     rsx = float(individual["rsx"])
4672:     rsy = float(individual["rsy"])
4673:     FILM_X_SHEET_RESISTANCE = rsx
4674:     FILM_Y_SHEET_RESISTANCE = rsy
4675: 
4676:     rsx_name = str(round(rsx, 2)).replace(".", "p")
4677:     rsy_name = str(round(rsy, 2)).replace(".", "p")
4678:     FILM_X_SHEET_NAME = f"SMH188H_X_{rsx_name}_OhmicSheet"
4679:     FILM_Y_SHEET_NAME = f"SMH188H_Y_{rsy_name}_OhmicSheet"
4680: 
4681: 
4682: def run_cst(individual, film_model_type=None):
4683:     if film_model_type is None:
4684:         film_model_type = OPTIMIZATION_FILM_MODEL
4685:     configure_rs_for_individual(individual)
4686:     p, h, mask_x_seed, mask_y_seed = decode(individual)
4687:     return run_cst_for_params(
4688:         p=p,
4689:         h=h,
4690:         mask_x_seed=mask_x_seed,
4691:         mask_y_seed=mask_y_seed,
4692:         film_model_type=film_model_type,
4693:     )
4694: 
4695: 
4696: def _find_effective_intervals(freqs, rls, threshold=-10.0):
4697:     below = rls <= threshold
4698:     intervals = []
4699:     start = None
4700:     for i, ok in enumerate(below):
4701:         if ok and start is None:
4702:             start = i
4703:         if (not ok or i == len(below) - 1) and start is not None:
4704:             end = i - 1 if not ok else i
4705:             if end >= start:
4706:                 intervals.append((start, end))
4707:             start = None
4708:     return intervals
4709: 
4710: 
4711: def extract_metrics(cst_file):
4712:     result = cst.results.ProjectFile(cst_file, allow_interactive=True)
4713:     s11 = result.get_3d().get_result_item(r"1D Results\S-Parameters\SZmax(1),Zmax(1)")
4714:     data = s11.get_data()
4715: 
4716:     freqs, rls = [], []
4717:     for row in data:
4718:         if len(row) < 2:
4719:             continue
4720:         freq = float(row[0])
4721:         mag = max(abs(row[1]), 1e-12)
4722:         freqs.append(freq)
4723:         rls.append(float(20 * np.log10(mag)))
4724: 
4725:     if not freqs:
4726:         return {"score": 0.0, "bandwidth": 0.0, "coverage": 0.0, "min_rl": 0.0, "f_low": 18.0, "f_high": 2.0}
4727: 
4728:     freqs = np.array(freqs, dtype=float)
4729:     rls = np.array(rls, dtype=float)
4730:     intervals = _find_effective_intervals(freqs, rls, -10.0)
4731: 
4732:     if intervals:
4733:         i0, i1 = max(intervals, key=lambda t: freqs[t[1]] - freqs[t[0]])
4734:         f_low = float(freqs[i0])
4735:         f_high = float(freqs[i1])
4736:         bw = max(0.0, f_high - f_low)
4737:     else:
4738:         f_low, f_high, bw = 18.0, 2.0, 0.0
4739: 
4740:     min_rl = float(np.min(rls))
4741:     df = float(np.mean(np.diff(freqs))) if len(freqs) > 1 else 0.0
4742:     below = rls <= -10.0
4743:     total_effective_bw = float(np.sum(below) * df)
4744: 
4745:     if np.any(below):
4746:         margin = -10.0 - rls[below]
4747:         mean_margin = float(np.mean(np.maximum(0.0, margin)))
4748:         shallow_ratio = float(np.mean((rls[below] > -11.0).astype(float)))
4749:     else:
4750:         mean_margin = 0.0
4751:         shallow_ratio = 1.0
4752: 
4753:     score = 3.2 * bw + 0.20 * total_effective_bw
4754: 
4755:     # Main target: keep high end and push low edge lower.
4756:     if f_high >= 17.90:
4757:         score += 14.0
4758:     else:
4759:         score -= 9.0 * (17.90 - f_high)
4760: 
4761:     if f_low <= 5.00:
4762:         score += 8.0 + 4.0 * (5.00 - f_low)
4763:     else:
4764:         score -= 7.0 * (f_low - 5.00)
4765: 
4766:     if f_low <= 4.80:
4767:         score += 7.0
4768:     if f_low <= 4.60:
4769:         score += 10.0
4770:     if f_low <= 4.45:
4771:         score += 14.0
4772: 
4773:     # Reference and target bonuses.
4774:     if bw >= REFERENCE_9X9_BW:
4775:         score += 6.0
4776:     if bw >= REFERENCE_12X12_BW:
4777:         score += 14.0 + 4.0 * (bw - REFERENCE_12X12_BW)
4778:     if bw >= 13.35:
4779:         score += 16.0
4780:     if bw >= TARGET_85_BW:
4781:         score += 45.0 + 8.0 * (bw - TARGET_85_BW)
4782: 
4783:     # Avoid a barely below -10 dB curve.
4784:     score += 0.05 * min(abs(min_rl), 50.0)
4785:     score += 0.10 * min(mean_margin, 18.0)
4786:     score -= 2.0 * shallow_ratio
4787: 
4788:     # Penalize very poor bandwidth.
4789:     if bw < 12.0:
4790:         score -= 12.0
4791: 
4792:     return {
4793:         "score": float(score),
4794:         "bandwidth": float(bw),
4795:         "coverage": float(bw / TARGET_TOTAL_BAND),
4796:         "min_rl": min_rl,
4797:         "f_low": f_low,
4798:         "f_high": f_high,
4799:         "contiguous_bw": float(bw),
4800:         "total_effective_bw": total_effective_bw,
4801:         "mean_margin": mean_margin,
4802:         "shallow_ratio": shallow_ratio,
4803:     }
4804: 
4805: 
4806: # -------------------------
4807: # Mutation operators
4808: # -------------------------
4809: def random_ph_rs_near_best(wide=False):
4810:     if wide:
4811:         p = random.uniform(P_MIN, P_MAX)
4812:         h = random.uniform(H_MIN, H_MAX)
4813:     else:
4814:         p = random.gauss(BASE_P_12, 0.13)
4815:         h = random.gauss(BASE_H_12, 0.16)
4816:     p = max(P_MIN, min(P_MAX, p))
4817:     h = max(H_MIN, min(H_MAX, h))
4818: 
4819:     rsx = random.gauss(BASE_RSX_12, 25.0)
4820:     rsy = random.gauss(BASE_RSY_12, 9.0)
4821:     if wide:
4822:         rsx = random.choice([random.uniform(100, 180), random.uniform(150, RS_X_MAX), random.uniform(RS_X_MIN, RS_X_MAX)])
4823:         rsy = random.choice([random.uniform(25, 45), random.uniform(35, 65), random.uniform(RS_Y_MIN, RS_Y_MAX)])
4824: 
4825:     rsx = max(RS_X_MIN, min(RS_X_MAX, rsx))
4826:     rsy = max(RS_Y_MIN, min(RS_Y_MAX, rsy))
4827:     return round(p, 4), round(h, 4), round(rsx, 3), round(rsy, 3)
4828: 
4829: 
4830: def point_flip(mask_x, mask_y, layer=None, n_flips=None):
4831:     mx = ensure_array_mask(mask_x).copy()
4832:     my = ensure_array_mask(mask_y).copy()
4833:     if n_flips is None:
4834:         n_flips = random.randint(LOCAL_FLIP_MIN, LOCAL_FLIP_MAX)
4835:     if layer is None:
4836:         layer = "Y" if random.random() < Y_MUTATION_PROB else "X"
4837: 
4838:     for _ in range(n_flips):
4839:         if layer == "X":
4840:             i, j = random.choice(X_SOFT_POS)
4841:             mx[i, j] = 1 - mx[i, j]
4842:         else:
4843:             if Y_SOFT_POS:
4844:                 i, j = random.choice(Y_SOFT_POS)
4845:                 my[i, j] = 1 - my[i, j]
4846:     return repair_masks(mx, my)
4847: 
4848: 
4849: def block_mutation(mask_x, mask_y, layer=None):
4850:     mx = ensure_array_mask(mask_x).copy()
4851:     my = ensure_array_mask(mask_y).copy()
4852:     if layer is None:
4853:         layer = "Y" if random.random() < Y_MUTATION_PROB else "X"
4854: 
4855:     bh, bw = random.choice([(1,2), (2,1), (2,2), (2,3), (3,2)])
4856:     setval = random.randint(0, 1)
4857: 
4858:     if layer == "X":
4859:         i = random.randint(3, 8)
4860:         j = random.randint(0, 12 - bw)
4861:         mx[i:i+bh, j:j+bw] = setval
4862:     else:
4863:         # choose a soft-region center
4864:         if not Y_SOFT_POS:
4865:             return mx, my
4866:         ci, cj = random.choice(Y_SOFT_POS)
4867:         i = max(1, min(11 - bh, ci - bh // 2))
4868:         j = max(1, min(11 - bw, cj - bw // 2))
4869:         block_mask = np.zeros_like(my, dtype=bool)
4870:         block_mask[i:i+bh, j:j+bw] = True
4871:         block_mask[Y_HARD_KEEP] = False
4872:         my[block_mask] = setval
4873: 
4874:     return repair_masks(mx, my)
4875: 
4876: 
4877: def edge_grow_shrink(mask_x, mask_y, layer=None, grow=None):
4878:     mx = ensure_array_mask(mask_x).copy()
4879:     my = ensure_array_mask(mask_y).copy()
4880:     if layer is None:
4881:         layer = "Y" if random.random() < Y_MUTATION_PROB else "X"
4882:     if grow is None:
4883:         grow = random.random() < 0.58
4884: 
4885:     m = mx if layer == "X" else my
4886:     positions = X_SOFT_POS if layer == "X" else Y_SOFT_POS
4887:     if not positions:
4888:         return mx, my
4889: 
4890:     candidates = []
4891:     for i, j in positions:
4892:         if layer == "Y" and Y_HARD_KEEP[i, j]:
4893:             continue
4894:         val = m[i, j]
4895:         neighbor_one = False
4896:         neighbor_zero = False
4897:         for di, dj in [(-1,0),(1,0),(0,-1),(0,1)]:
4898:             ii, jj = i + di, j + dj
4899:             if 0 <= ii < 12 and 0 <= jj < 12:
4900:                 neighbor_one = neighbor_one or (m[ii, jj] == 1)
4901:                 neighbor_zero = neighbor_zero or (m[ii, jj] == 0)
4902:         if grow and val == 0 and neighbor_one:
4903:             candidates.append((i, j))
4904:         if (not grow) and val == 1 and neighbor_zero:
4905:             candidates.append((i, j))
4906: 
4907:     if candidates:
4908:         for _ in range(random.randint(1, 4)):
4909:             i, j = random.choice(candidates)
4910:             if layer == "X":
4911:                 mx[i, j] = 1 if grow else 0
4912:             else:
4913:                 if not Y_HARD_KEEP[i, j]:
4914:                     my[i, j] = 1 if grow else 0
4915: 
4916:     return repair_masks(mx, my)
4917: 
4918: 
4919: def bridge_or_slot_mutation(mask_x, mask_y):
4920:     mx = ensure_array_mask(mask_x).copy()
4921:     my = ensure_array_mask(mask_y).copy()
4922: 
4923:     if random.random() < 0.55:
4924:         # X bridge/slot around the central aperture and right loading zone
4925:         mode = random.randint(0, 5)
4926:         if mode == 0:
4927:             mx[4, 5:8] = random.choice([0, 1])
4928:         elif mode == 1:
4929:             mx[8, 6:9] = random.choice([0, 1])
4930:         elif mode == 2:
4931:             mx[5:8, 4] = random.choice([0, 1])
4932:         elif mode == 3:
4933:             mx[5:8, 8] = random.choice([0, 1])
4934:         elif mode == 4:
4935:             mx[4:9, 1] = random.choice([0, 1])
4936:         else:
4937:             mx[4:9, 10] = random.choice([0, 1])
4938:     else:
4939:         # Y local slot/bridge in soft window regions
4940:         mode = random.randint(0, 5)
4941:         if mode == 0:
4942:             my[3, 4:9] = random.choice([0, 1])
4943:         elif mode == 1:
4944:             my[8, 3:9] = random.choice([0, 1])
4945:         elif mode == 2:
4946:             my[4:9, 5] = random.choice([0, 1])
4947:         elif mode == 3:
4948:             my[4:9, 8] = random.choice([0, 1])
4949:         elif mode == 4:
4950:             my[1:4, 9] = random.choice([0, 1])
4951:         else:
4952:             my[9:11, 9] = random.choice([0, 1])
4953:         my[Y_HARD_KEEP] = 1
4954: 
4955:     return repair_masks(mx, my)
4956: 
4957: 
4958: def apply_random_topology_mutations(ind, n_ops=None, origin="mutated"):
4959:     child = clone_individual(ind)
4960:     mx = ensure_array_mask(child["mask_x"])
4961:     my = ensure_array_mask(child["mask_y"])
4962: 
4963:     if n_ops is None:
4964:         n_ops = random.randint(1, 4)
4965: 
4966:     for _ in range(n_ops):
4967:         r = random.random()
4968:         if r < 0.35:
4969:             mx, my = point_flip(mx, my, n_flips=random.randint(1, 5))
4970:         elif r < 0.35 + BLOCK_MUTATION_PROB:
4971:             mx, my = block_mutation(mx, my)
4972:         elif r < 0.35 + BLOCK_MUTATION_PROB + EDGE_MUTATION_PROB:
4973:             mx, my = edge_grow_shrink(mx, my)
4974:         else:
4975:             mx, my = bridge_or_slot_mutation(mx, my)
4976: 
4977:     child["mask_x"] = mx.astype(int).tolist()
4978:     child["mask_y"] = my.astype(int).tolist()
4979:     child["origin"] = origin
4980:     return child
4981: 
4982: 
4983: def mutate_params(ind, wide=False):
4984:     child = clone_individual(ind)
4985:     scale = 1.8 if wide else 1.0
4986:     child["p"] = round(max(P_MIN, min(P_MAX, float(child["p"]) + random.gauss(0, 0.055 * scale))), 4)
4987:     child["h"] = round(max(H_MIN, min(H_MAX, float(child["h"]) + random.gauss(0, 0.075 * scale))), 4)
4988:     child["rsx"] = round(max(RS_X_MIN, min(RS_X_MAX, float(child["rsx"]) + random.gauss(0, 7.0 * scale))), 3)
4989:     child["rsy"] = round(max(RS_Y_MIN, min(RS_Y_MAX, float(child["rsy"]) + random.gauss(0, 3.8 * scale))), 3)
4990:     return child
4991:

4996: def make_best82_seed(origin="best82_seed"):
4997:     return make_individual(
4998:         p=BASE_P_12, h=BASE_H_12, rsx=BASE_RSX_12, rsy=BASE_RSY_12,
4999:         mask_x=BEST82_X_MASK_12, mask_y=BEST82_Y_MASK_12, origin=origin,
5000:     )
5001: 
5002: 
5003: def make_ph_rs_seed(origin="ph_rs_seed"):
5004:     p, h, rsx, rsy = random_ph_rs_near_best(wide=True)
5005:     return make_individual(p=p, h=h, rsx=rsx, rsy=rsy, mask_x=BEST82_X_MASK_12, mask_y=BEST82_Y_MASK_12, origin=origin)
5006: 
5007: 
5008: def make_lowfreq_template(origin="lowfreq_template"):
5009:     # Lower low edge: slightly denser Y and longer X arms, with h around 10.1.
5010:     mx = BEST82_X_MASK_12.copy()
5011:     my = BEST82_Y_MASK_12.copy()
5012: 
5013:     # Y: grow a few central/side soft edges.
5014:     for pos in random.sample(Y_SOFT_POS, min(len(Y_SOFT_POS), random.randint(4, 10))):
5015:         if random.random() < 0.65:
5016:             my[pos] = 1
5017: 
5018:     # X: add longer left/right high-frequency arms, but not too dense.
5019:     if random.random() < 0.5:
5020:         mx[4:8, 0] = 1
5021:         mx[4:8, 11] = 1
5022:     if random.random() < 0.5:
5023:         mx[8, 7:10] = 1
5024: 
5025:     p = random.uniform(12.95, 13.25)
5026:     h = random.uniform(10.00, 10.35)
5027:     rsx = random.uniform(125, 200)
5028:     rsy = random.uniform(25, 45)
5029:     return make_individual(p=p, h=h, rsx=rsx, rsy=rsy, mask_x=mx, mask_y=my, origin=origin)
5030: 
5031: 
5032: def make_hf_template(origin="hf_template"):
5033:     # High-frequency compensation: thinner X and more slots; Y slightly less lossy.
5034:     mx = BEST82_X_MASK_12.copy()
5035:     my = BEST82_Y_MASK_12.copy()
5036: 
5037:     # X slots around central aperture
5038:     mx[5:8, 4] = 0
5039:     mx[5:8, 8] = random.choice([0, 1])
5040:     if random.random() < 0.5:
5041:         mx[4, 5:8] = 1
5042:     if random.random() < 0.5:
5043:         mx[8, 7:9] = 1
5044: 
5045:     # Y: create/adjust local slots without breaking hard skeleton.
5046:     for _ in range(random.randint(2, 6)):
5047:         i, j = random.choice(Y_SOFT_POS)
5048:         my[i, j] = random.choice([0, 1])
5049:     my[Y_HARD_KEEP] = 1
5050: 
5051:     p = random.uniform(13.00, 13.30)
5052:     h = random.uniform(9.70, 10.12)
5053:     rsx = random.uniform(140, 230)
5054:     rsy = random.uniform(30, 55)
5055:     return make_individual(p=p, h=h, rsx=rsx, rsy=rsy, mask_x=mx, mask_y=my, origin=origin)
5056: 
5057: 
5058: def make_bridge_template(origin="bridge_template"):
5059:     # Mid-band flattening: bridges between low/high response paths.
5060:     mx = BEST82_X_MASK_12.copy()
5061:     my = BEST82_Y_MASK_12.copy()
5062: 
5063:     # X: add local bridges around the central window.
5064:     mx[4, 4] = 1
5065:     mx[4, 8] = 1
5066:     mx[8, 7:9] = 1
5067:     if random.random() < 0.5:
5068:         mx[5:8, 5] = 1
5069:     if random.random() < 0.5:
5070:         mx[5:8, 7] = 1
5071: 
5072:     # Y: adjust center cross/window edges.
5073:     for pos in [(3,4), (3,5), (3,8), (8,4), (8,5), (8,8), (9,8)]:
5074:         if not Y_HARD_KEEP[pos]:
5075:             my[pos] = random.choice([0, 1])
5076:     my[Y_HARD_KEEP] = 1
5077: 
5078:     p = random.uniform(13.00, 13.35)
5079:     h = random.uniform(9.85, 10.25)
5080:     rsx = random.uniform(115, 190)
5081:     rsy = random.uniform(25, 55)
5082:     return make_individual(p=p, h=h, rsx=rsx, rsy=rsy, mask_x=mx, mask_y=my, origin=origin)
5083: 
5084: 
5085: def make_random_multitemplate_seed():
5086:     r = random.random()
5087:     if r < 0.25:
5088:         return make_ph_rs_seed(origin="ph_rs_seed")
5089:     if r < 0.50:
5090:         return make_lowfreq_template(origin="lowfreq_template")
5091:     if r < 0.75:
5092:         return make_hf_template(origin="hf_template")
5093:     return make_bridge_template(origin="bridge_template")

5096: # -------------------------
5097: # Optimization helper functions
5098: # -------------------------
5099: def log_individual_trace(generation, index, status, individual, reason="", report=None, metrics=None, cst_file=""):
5100:     try:
5101:         p, h, sx, sy = decode(individual)
5102:         mx = expand_seed_to_mask(sx)
5103:         my = expand_seed_to_mask(sy)
5104:         if report is None:
5105:             report = {
5106:                 "x": mask_quality_report(mx),
5107:                 "y": mask_quality_report(my),
5108:                 "total_kept": int(sum(sum(r) for r in mx) + sum(sum(r) for r in my)),
5109:             }
5110:         metric_str = ""
5111:         if metrics is not None:
5112:             metric_str = (
5113:                 f"\tscore={metrics.get('score', '')}"
5114:                 f"\tbandwidth={metrics.get('bandwidth', '')}"
5115:                 f"\tcoverage={metrics.get('coverage', 0.0)}"
5116:                 f"\tmin_rl={metrics.get('min_rl', '')}"
5117:                 f"\tf_low={metrics.get('f_low', '')}"
5118:                 f"\tf_high={metrics.get('f_high', '')}"
5119:             )
5120:         line = (
5121:             f"gen={generation}\tidx={index}\tstatus={status}\treason={reason}\t"
5122:             f"summary={template_summary(individual)}\t"
5123:             f"mask_x={mask_to_compact_string(mx)}\tmask_y={mask_to_compact_string(my)}\t"
5124:             f"x_report={report.get('x')}\ty_report={report.get('y')}\t"
5125:             f"total_kept={report.get('total_kept')}\tcst_file={cst_file}{metric_str}\n"
5126:         )
5127:         with open(INDIVIDUAL_TRACE_FILE, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
5128:             f.write(line)
5129:     except Exception as e:
5130:         with open(INDIVIDUAL_TRACE_FILE, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
5131:             f.write(f"gen={generation}\tidx={index}\tstatus=trace_failed\terror={repr(e)}\n")
5132: 
5133: 
5134: def evaluate_individual(individual, generation=None, index=None):
5135:     key = individual_key(individual)
5136:     if key in cache:
5137:         metrics, params, cst_file = cache[key]
5138:         log_individual_trace(generation, index, "cache_hit", individual, metrics=metrics, cst_file=cst_file)
5139:         return cache[key]
5140: 
5141:     ok, reason, report = prefilter_individual(individual)
5142:     if not ok:
5143:         metrics = {"score": 0.0, "bandwidth": 0.0, "coverage": 0.0, "min_rl": 0.0, "f_low": 18.0, "f_high": 2.0}
5144:         p, h, sx, sy = decode(individual)
5145:         params = (p, h, sx, sy)
5146:         cst_file = ""
5147:         cache[key] = (metrics, params, cst_file)
5148:         log_individual_trace(generation, index, "skipped_prefilter", individual, reason=reason, report=report, metrics=metrics)
5149:         with open(SKIPPED_INDIVIDUAL_FILE, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
5150:             f.write(f"gen={generation}\tidx={index}\treason={reason}\tsummary={template_summary(individual)}\n")
5151:         return cache[key]
5152: 
5153:     log_individual_trace(generation, index, "start_cst", individual, reason=reason, report=report)
5154:     try:
5155:         metrics, params, cst_file = run_cst(individual)
5156:         log_individual_trace(generation, index, "finished_cst", individual, reason="ok", report=report, metrics=metrics, cst_file=cst_file)
5157:     except Exception as e:
5158:         with open(ERROR_LOG_FILE, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
5159:             f.write("\n" + "=" * 80 + "\n")
5160:             f.write(f"generation = {generation}, index = {index}\n")
5161:             f.write(f"summary = {template_summary(individual)}\n")
5162:             f.write(f"prefilter_report = {report}\n")
5163:             f.write(repr(e) + "\n")
5164:             f.write(traceback.format_exc() + "\n")
5165:         metrics = {"score": 0.0, "bandwidth": 0.0, "coverage": 0.0, "min_rl": 0.0, "f_low": 18.0, "f_high": 2.0}
5166:         p, h, sx, sy = decode(individual)
5167:         params = (p, h, sx, sy)
5168:         cst_file = ""
5169:         log_individual_trace(generation, index, "failed_cst", individual, reason=repr(e), report=report, metrics=metrics)
5170: 
5171:     cache[key] = (metrics, params, cst_file)
5172:     return cache[key]
5173: 
5174: 
5175: def evaluate_population(pop, generation=None):
5176:     fit_list = []
5177:     info_list = []
5178:     for idx, ind in enumerate(pop):
5179:         print(f"\n--- Evaluating generation {generation}, individual {idx + 1}/{len(pop)} ---")
5180:         print(template_summary(ind))
5181:         metrics, params, cst_file = evaluate_individual(ind, generation=generation, index=idx)
5182:         fit_list.append(metrics["score"])
5183:         info_list.append((metrics, params, cst_file))
5184:     return fit_list, info_list
5185: 
5186: 
5187: def select_parent(pop, fit_list):
5188:     positive = [max(0.0, x) for x in fit_list]
5189:     total = sum(positive)
5190:     if total <= 0:
5191:         return clone_individual(random.choice(pop))
5192:     r = random.random() * total
5193:     s = 0.0
5194:     for ind, fit in zip(pop, positive):
5195:         s += fit
5196:         if r <= s:
5197:             return clone_individual(ind)
5198:     return clone_individual(pop[-1])
5199: 
5200: 
5201: def crossover(father, mother):
5202:     fx, fy = ensure_array_mask(father["mask_x"]), ensure_array_mask(father["mask_y"])
5203:     mx, my = ensure_array_mask(mother["mask_x"]), ensure_array_mask(mother["mask_y"])
5204: 
5205:     child_x = fx.copy()
5206:     child_y = fy.copy()
5207: 
5208:     # X: block crossover in active rows.
5209:     for i in range(3, 10, 2):
5210:         for j in range(0, 12, 2):
5211:             if random.random() < 0.5:
5212:                 child_x[i:i+2, j:j+2] = mx[i:i+2, j:j+2]
5213: 
5214:     # Y: soft-region crossover only.
5215:     for i, j in Y_SOFT_POS:
5216:         if random.random() < 0.35:
5217:             child_y[i, j] = my[i, j]
5218: 
5219:     child_x, child_y = repair_masks(child_x, child_y)
5220: 
5221:     p = (float(father["p"]) + float(mother["p"])) / 2.0 + random.gauss(0, 0.045)
5222:     h = (float(father["h"]) + float(mother["h"])) / 2.0 + random.gauss(0, 0.060)
5223:     rsx = (float(father["rsx"]) + float(mother["rsx"])) / 2.0 + random.gauss(0, 5.0)
5224:     rsy = (float(father["rsy"]) + float(mother["rsy"])) / 2.0 + random.gauss(0, 2.8)
5225: 
5226:     return make_individual(p=p, h=h, rsx=rsx, rsy=rsy, mask_x=child_x, mask_y=child_y, origin="crossover")
5227: 
5228: 
5229: def mutate(individual, p_m):
5230:     child = clone_individual(individual)
5231: 
5232:     if random.random() < p_m:
5233:         child = mutate_params(child, wide=(random.random() < 0.20))
5234: 
5235:     if random.random() < p_m:
5236:         child = apply_random_topology_mutations(child, n_ops=random.randint(1, 4), origin="topology_mutated")
5237: 
5238:     return child
5239: 
5240: 
5241: def initialize_population(n):
5242:     pop = []
5243: 
5244:     # 1) Hard elites / references
5245:     pop.append(make_best82_seed(origin="best82_reference"))
5246: 
5247:     reference_settings = [
5248:         (13.1437, 10.0791, 142.198, 38.599),
5249:         (13.0574, 9.7319, 127.53, 44.144),
5250:         (13.1059, 9.7222, 151.094, 51.242),
5251:         (13.20, 10.00, 120.0, 55.0),
5252:         (13.10, 9.86, 160.0, 35.0),
5253:         (13.00, 10.18, 180.0, 32.0),
5254:     ]
5255:     for p, h, rsx, rsy in reference_settings:
5256:         if len(pop) >= n:
5257:             break
5258:         pop.append(make_individual(p=p, h=h, rsx=rsx, rsy=rsy, mask_x=BEST82_X_MASK_12, mask_y=BEST82_Y_MASK_12, origin="reference"))
5259: 
5260:     # 2) Multi-template seeds
5261:     trial = 0
5262:     while len(pop) < n and trial < RANDOM_INDIVIDUAL_MAX_TRIALS:
5263:         trial += 1
5264:         if random.random() < 0.55:
5265:             child = make_random_multitemplate_seed()
5266:         else:
5267:             parent = random.choice(pop)
5268:             child = apply_random_topology_mutations(parent, n_ops=random.randint(1, 5), origin="initial_mutated")
5269:             child = mutate_params(child, wide=(random.random() < 0.35))
5270: 
5271:         ok, reason, report = prefilter_individual(child)
5272:         if ok and individual_key(child) not in [individual_key(x) for x in pop]:
5273:             pop.append(child)
5274:         else:
5275:             with open(SKIPPED_INDIVIDUAL_FILE, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
5276:                 f.write(f"initial_skipped\treason={reason}\tsummary={template_summary(child)}\n")
5277: 
5278:     while len(pop) < n:
5279:         pop.append(mutate_params(make_best82_seed(origin="fill_best82")))
5280: 
5281:     return pop
5282: 
5283: 
5284: def make_new_population(pop, fit_list, p_m):
5285:     elite_idx = int(np.argmax(fit_list))
5286:     elite = clone_individual(pop[elite_idx])
5287:     newpop = [elite]
5288: 
5289:     # Always preserve the 82% known-good seed.
5290:     known_best = make_best82_seed(origin="best82_reference")
5291:     newpop.append(known_best)
5292: 
5293:     # Also preserve one parameter-only variation around the elite.
5294:     elite_param = mutate_params(elite, wide=False)
5295:     elite_param["origin"] = "elite_param_local"
5296:     newpop.append(elite_param)
5297: 
5298:     trial = 0
5299:     while len(newpop) < pop_size and trial < RANDOM_INDIVIDUAL_MAX_TRIALS:
5300:         trial += 1
5301:         r = random.random()
5302: 
5303:         if r < 0.42:
5304:             child = crossover(select_parent(pop, fit_list), select_parent(pop, fit_list))
5305:             child = mutate(child, p_m)
5306:         elif r < 0.66:
5307:             parent = select_parent(pop, fit_list)
5308:             child = apply_random_topology_mutations(parent, n_ops=random.randint(1, 5), origin="elite_topology_local")
5309:             child = mutate_params(child, wide=(random.random() < 0.18))
5310:         elif r < 0.84:
5311:             child = make_random_multitemplate_seed()
5312:         else:
5313:             child = make_ph_rs_seed(origin="ph_rs_injected")
5314: 
5315:         ok, reason, report = prefilter_individual(child)
5316:         if ok and individual_key(child) not in [individual_key(x) for x in newpop]:
5317:             newpop.append(child)
5318:         else:
5319:             with open(SKIPPED_INDIVIDUAL_FILE, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
5320:                 f.write(f"new_population_skipped\treason={reason}\tsummary={template_summary(child)}\n")
5321: 
5322:     while len(newpop) < pop_size:
5323:         child = apply_random_topology_mutations(elite, n_ops=1, origin="fill_elite_local")
5324:         child = mutate_params(child, wide=False)
5325:         newpop.append(child)
5326: 
5327:     return newpop
5328: 
5329: 
5330: def save_mask_txt(mask, path):
5331:     m = ensure_array_mask(mask)
5332:     with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
5333:         for row in m:
5334:             f.write(" ".join(str(int(v)) for v in row) + "\n")
5335: 
5336: 
5337: def export_mask_figure(mask_x, mask_y, out_png):
5338:     mx = ensure_array_mask(mask_x)
5339:     my = ensure_array_mask(mask_y)
5340:     fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(8, 4), constrained_layout=True)
5341:     for ax, mask, title in [
5342:         (axes[0], mx, "X-oriented 12x12 multi-template pattern"),
5343:         (axes[1], my, "Y-oriented semi-constrained 12x12 pattern"),
5344:     ]:
5345:         ax.imshow(mask, cmap="gray_r", vmin=0, vmax=1)
5346:         ax.set_title(title)
5347:         ax.set_xticks(range(12))
5348:         ax.set_yticks(range(12))
5349:         ax.set_xticklabels(range(1, 13), fontsize=6)
5350:         ax.set_yticklabels(range(1, 13), fontsize=6)
5351:         ax.set_xlabel("Pixel column")
5352:         ax.set_ylabel("Pixel row")
5353:         ax.set_xticks(np.arange(-0.5, 12, 1), minor=True)
5354:         ax.set_yticks(np.arange(-0.5, 12, 1), minor=True)
5355:         ax.grid(which="minor", color="black", linewidth=0.5)
5356:         ax.tick_params(which="minor", bottom=False, left=False)
5357:     fig.suptitle("Best 12x12 semi-constrained multi-template Ohmic-sheet topology")
5358:     fig.savefig(out_png, dpi=300, bbox_inches="tight")
5359:     plt.close(fig)
5360: 
5361: 
5362: def write_best_result(best_metrics, best_ind, best_params):
5363:     with open(BEST_RESULT_FILE, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
5364:         f.write("12x12 semi-constrained multi-template topology GA result\n")
5365:         f.write(f"Best score: {best_metrics['score']}\n")
5366:         f.write(f"Best contiguous bandwidth( RL<-10 dB ): {best_metrics['bandwidth']} GHz\n")
5367:         f.write(f"Best coverage in 2-18 GHz: {best_metrics.get('coverage', 0.0) * 100.0} %\n")
5368:         f.write(f"Best total effective bandwidth estimate: {best_metrics.get('total_effective_bw', 0.0)} GHz\n")
5369:         f.write(f"Best low edge: {best_metrics.get('f_low', 0.0)} GHz\n")
5370:         f.write(f"Best high edge: {best_metrics.get('f_high', 0.0)} GHz\n")
5371:         f.write(f"Best min RL: {best_metrics['min_rl']} dB\n")
5372:         f.write(f"Best mean margin below -10 dB: {best_metrics.get('mean_margin', 0.0)} dB\n")
5373:         f.write(f"Best individual summary: {template_summary(best_ind)}\n")
5374:         f.write(f"Best parameters (p, h, mask_x_flatten, mask_y_flatten): {best_params}\n")
5375:         f.write(f"Best CST file: {BEST_CST}\n")
5376:         f.write(f"Best S11 txt file: {BEST_S11_FILE}\n")
5377:         f.write(f"Best S11 png file: {BEST_S11_PNG}\n")
5378:         f.write(f"Best X mask txt file: {BEST_MASK_X_TXT}\n")
5379:         f.write(f"Best Y mask txt file: {BEST_MASK_Y_TXT}\n")
5380:         f.write(f"Best mask figure: {BEST_MASK_PNG}\n\n")
5381:         f.write("Best X mask compact:\n")
5382:         f.write(matrix_to_string(best_ind["mask_x"]) + "\n\n")
5383:         f.write("Best Y mask compact:\n")
5384:         f.write(matrix_to_string(best_ind["mask_y"]) + "\n")
5385: 
5386: 
5387: def optimize():
5388:     global cache
5389:     cache = {}
5390: 
5391:     for path in [LOG_FILE, ERROR_LOG_FILE, INDIVIDUAL_TRACE_FILE, SKIPPED_INDIVIDUAL_FILE]:
5392:         try:
5393:             if os.path.exists(path):
5394:                 os.remove(path)
5395:         except Exception:
5396:             pass
5397: 
5398:     pop = initialize_population(pop_size)
5399:     best_metrics = {"score": -1.0, "bandwidth": 0.0, "coverage": 0.0, "min_rl": 0.0}
5400:     best_ind = None
5401:     best_params = None
5402: 
5403:     with open(LOG_FILE, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
5404:         f.write("iteration\tindex\tsummary\tscore\tbandwidth\tcoverage\tmin_rl\tf_low\tf_high\ttotal_effective_bw\tmean_margin\tp\th\trs_x\trs_y\tcst_file\n")
5405: 
5406:     for it in range(iterations):
5407:         current_pm = max(0.07, p_m_start - 0.014 * it)
5408:         print(f"\n===== Multi-template topology Iteration {it} =====")
5409:         print(f"Current mutation rate = {current_pm:.3f}")
5410: 
5411:         fit_list, info_list = evaluate_population(pop, generation=it)
5412: 
5413:         for idx, (ind, fit, info) in enumerate(zip(pop, fit_list, info_list)):
5414:             metrics, params, cst_file = info
5415:             print(
5416:                 f"score = {metrics['score']:.6f}, "
5417:                 f"bandwidth = {metrics['bandwidth']:.6f}, "
5418:                 f"coverage = {metrics.get('coverage', 0.0)*100:.2f}%, "
5419:                 f"f_low = {metrics.get('f_low', 0.0):.3f}, "
5420:                 f"f_high = {metrics.get('f_high', 0.0):.3f}, "
5421:                 f"minRL = {metrics['min_rl']:.3f}"
5422:             )
5423:             with open(LOG_FILE, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
5424:                 f.write(
5425:                     f"{it}\t{idx}\t{template_summary(ind)}\t{metrics['score']}\t{metrics['bandwidth']}\t"
5426:                     f"{metrics.get('coverage', 0.0)}\t{metrics['min_rl']}\t{metrics.get('f_low', 0.0)}\t"
5427:                     f"{metrics.get('f_high', 0.0)}\t{metrics.get('total_effective_bw', 0.0)}\t"
5428:                     f"{metrics.get('mean_margin', 0.0)}\t{ind['p']}\t{ind['h']}\t"
5429:                     f"{ind['rsx']}\t{ind['rsy']}\t{cst_file}\n"
5430:                 )
5431: 
5432:         idx = int(np.argmax(fit_list))
5433:         current_best_metrics, current_best_params, current_best_cst = info_list[idx]
5434:         if current_best_metrics["score"] > best_metrics["score"]:
5435:             best_metrics = current_best_metrics
5436:             best_ind = clone_individual(pop[idx])
5437:             best_params = current_best_params
5438: 
5439:             if current_best_cst and os.path.exists(current_best_cst):
5440:                 remove_file_if_exists(BEST_CST)
5441:                 shutil.copyfile(current_best_cst, BEST_CST)
5442:                 export_s11_curve(current_best_cst, BEST_S11_FILE)
5443:                 export_s11_plot(current_best_cst, BEST_S11_PNG)
5444: 
5445:             save_mask_txt(best_ind["mask_x"], BEST_MASK_X_TXT)
5446:             save_mask_txt(best_ind["mask_y"], BEST_MASK_Y_TXT)
5447:             export_mask_figure(best_ind["mask_x"], best_ind["mask_y"], BEST_MASK_PNG)
5448:             write_best_result(best_metrics, best_ind, best_params)
5449: 
5450:         print(f"best score so far = {best_metrics['score']:.6f}")
5451:         print(f"best bandwidth so far = {best_metrics['bandwidth']:.6f} GHz, coverage = {best_metrics.get('coverage', 0.0)*100:.2f}%")
5452:         print(f"best f_low / f_high so far = {best_metrics.get('f_low', 0.0):.3f} / {best_metrics.get('f_high', 0.0):.3f} GHz")
5453:         print(f"best min RL so far = {best_metrics['min_rl']:.3f} dB")
5454:         print(f"best summary so far = {template_summary(best_ind) if best_ind else ''}")
5455: 
5456:         pop = make_new_population(pop, fit_list, current_pm)
5457: 
5458:     print("\nMulti-template topology optimization finished.")
5459:     print(f"Best score = {best_metrics['score']}")
5460:     print(f"Best bandwidth = {best_metrics['bandwidth']} GHz, coverage = {best_metrics.get('coverage', 0.0)*100:.2f}%")
5461:     print(f"Best f_low / f_high = {best_metrics.get('f_low', 0.0)} / {best_metrics.get('f_high', 0.0)} GHz")
5462:     print(f"Best min RL = {best_metrics['min_rl']} dB")
5463:     print(f"Best summary = {template_summary(best_ind) if best_ind else ''}")
5464:     print(f"Best CST file saved to: {BEST_CST}")
5465: 
5466: # =========================================================
5467: # END SEMI-CONSTRAINED MULTI-TEMPLATE TOPOLOGY GA BLOCK
